迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

吉里什·纳拉扬斯瓦米Girish Narayanswamy[°][,][†][,1,3][◦][,][†][,1,3], 麦克斯韦·徐, Maxwell A. Xu[°][,][†][,1,5][◦][,][†][,1,5], A·阿里·海达里, A. Ali Heydari[‡][,1] , 萨米·阿卜杜勒-加法尔, Samy Abdel-Ghaffar[‡][,1] 、马吕斯·盖拉德, Marius Guerard[‡][,1] , 卡拉·瓦扬古, Kara Vaillancourt[‡][,1] , 张志涵, Zhihan Zhang[‡][,1,3] , 杰克·加里森, Jake Garrison[‡][,1] , 莱维·阿尔伯克基, Levi Albuquerque[‡][,1] , 迪米特里斯·斯帕西斯, Dimitris Spathis[‡][,1] , 洪宇, Hong Yu[‡][,1] , 哈米德·帕兰吉, Hamid Palangi[‡][,1] , 徐海“Orson”徐, Xuhai "Orson" Xu[1] , 大卫·G.T.巴雷特, David G.T. Barrett[2] , 约瑟夫·布雷达, Joseph Breda[1] , 杰德·麦吉芬, Jed McGiffin[1,3] , 金玉彬, Yubin Kim[1] , 张宇伟, Yuwei Zhang[1] , 纳格梅·雷扎伊, Naghmeh Rezaei[1] , 塞缪尔·所罗门, Samuel Solomon[1] , 卡兰·阿胡贾, Karan Ahuja[1] , 蒂姆·阿尔索夫, Tim Althoff[1] , 杰克·阳光, Jake Sunshine[1,3] , 蒲明哲, Ming-Zher Poh[1] , 本杰明·耶顿, Benjamin Yetton[1] , 阿里·温布什, Ari Winbush[4] , 尼古拉斯·B·艾伦, Nicholas B. Rehg[5] 艾萨克·加拉策-利维, Isaac Galatzer-Levy[2] , 刘云, Yun Liu[1] , 约翰·埃尔南德斯, John Hernandez[1] , 阿努帕姆·帕塔克, Anupam Pathak[1] , 康纳·赫内根, Conor Heneghan[1] , 杨雨哲, Yuzhe Yang[1] 艾哈迈德·A·梅特瓦利, Ahmed A. ◦Co-first, △Co-last, ‡核心贡献者, †通讯作者, 1Google Research, 2Google DeepMind, 3华盛顿大学, 4俄勒冈大学, 5伊利诺伊大学厄巴纳-香槟分校◦Co-first, △Co-last, ‡Core Contributor, †Corresponding Author, 1Google Research, 2Google DeepMind, 3University of Washington, 4University of Oregon, 5University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign

虽然无处不在的可穿戴传感器可以捕获大量的行为和生理信息，但将这些信号有效地转化为个性化的健康见解仍然具有挑战性。具体来说，由于表型多样性以及个体基线健康、生理和生活方式因素的变化，将低级传感器数据转换为能够表征高级状态的表示是很困难的。此外，收集与健康结果注释配对的可穿戴数据既费力又昂贵，而且回顾性注释实际上仍然不可行，导致具有高质量标签的数据稀缺。为了克服这些限制，我们提出了一种可穿戴健康的基础模型，该模型经过了超过While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than 一万亿分钟one trillion minutes 从五百万参与者的大群体中提取的未标记的传感器信号。我们证明，模型容量和预训练数据量的联合扩展可以系统性地提高性能，这是对 35 项健康预测任务的评估结果，涵盖心血管、代谢、睡眠和心理健康，以及生活方式选择和人口统计因素。我们发现这种群体规模表示解锁了标签高效的小样本学习和生成能力，以实现稳健的日常指标估计。为了进一步利用这种学习到的表示，我们部署了一个 LLM 代理教室来自动搜索基于模型嵌入的下游预测头的空间，显示出随 LLM 模型容量而增加的广泛性能改进。最后，我们展示了如何将这些下游预测因子集成到个人健康代理中，以支持更相关、更情境感知和更安全的模型响应，并且我们通过一组临床医生的 1,860 个评级来验证这一点。of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.

一、简介1. Introduction

健康是多维的。它涵盖了从我们许多生物系统的功能到抽象的精神状态到总体幸福感的一切。从历史上看，收集和处理向人们提供个性化健康见解所需的大量数据都在追求个性化医疗保健的过程中遇到了关键的技术瓶颈。可穿戴和移动健康技术的大规模采用提供了前所未有的机会，可以扩大个人健康见解的获取范围并促进向预防性护理的转变。这些设备以连续和纵向采样传感器流的形式捕获的数据现在可以比以往更容易、更准确地测量身体活动和行为（McDuff 等人，2025a；Munos 等人，2016；Ringeval 等人，2020）。此外，越来越多的证据表明，这些高分辨率数据流可能有助于疾病检测和表型分析（Metwally 等人，2026 年；Yang 等人，2022 年）、早期检测和监测（Perez 等人，2019 年）以及干预措施的实施（Shah 等人，2025 年）。然而，将低水平传感器数据转换为适合表征更高水平健康状态的表示形式具有挑战性。现代解决方案主要采用Health is multidimensional. It comprises everything from the functioning of our many biological systems to abstract mental states to general well-being. Historically, both gathering and processing the volume of data required to provide individualized health insights to people have presented key technological bottlenecks in the pursuit of personalized healthcare. The large-scale adoption of wearable and mobile health technologies presents an unprecedented opportunity to broaden access to personal health insights and facilitate a shift towards preventive care. The data captured by these devices, in the form of continuous and longitudinally sampled sensor streams, now enables more accessible and accurate measurements of physical activity and behavior than ever before (McDuff et al., 2025a; Munos et al., 2016; Ringeval et al., 2020). Furthermore, a growing body of evidence suggests that these high-resolution data streams may be useful in disease detection and phenotyping (Metwally et al., 2026; Yang et al., 2022), early detection and monitoring (Perez et al., 2019) and the delivery of interventions (Shah et al., 2025). However, converting low-level sensor data into representations amenable to characterizing higher-level health states is challenging. Modern solutions largely take

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

针对个人健康结果的定制监督算法的形式。不幸的是，这些算法由于依赖传感器数据和健康结果注释而受到阻碍，这些数据费力、昂贵且无法事后获取。之前的工作通常采取零敲碎打的方法，仅利用针对一小部分健康终点的几种传感方式。然而，这种方法无法代表人群水平的高度表型多样性，基线健康和生理学存在异质性，下游健康结果也不同。因此，目前尚不清楚是否可以从可穿戴传感器数据中学习可概括的特征，这些数据捕获对不同个人和健康应用有用的信息。the form of bespoke supervised algorithms for individual health outcomes. Unfortunately, these algorithms are hindered by their reliance on sensor data paired with health outcome annotations, which are laborious, expensive, and infeasible to source post-hoc. Prior work has often taken a piecemeal approach, utilizing only a few sensing modalities that target a small set of health endpoints. However, this approach falls short of being able to represent the high degree of phenotypic diversity at the population level, with heterogeneity in baseline health and physiology and disparate downstream health outcomes. As such, it is yet unclear whether generalizable features can be learned from wearable sensor data that capture information useful for diverse individuals and health applications.

基础模型是机器学习的最新进展，有望解决这些限制并加速该领域的进展。这些模型通常利用大规模、异构、未标记数据集上的自监督学习 (SSL) 来学习通用表示，从而可以推广到广泛的任务。在健康领域，这些方法成功地提高了各种应用的性能，包括放射学（Wu et al., 2025）、病理学（Xu et al., 2024）和医学推理（McDuff et al., 2025b；Sellergren et al., 2025）。此外，早期的时间序列基础模型（Garza 等人，2023 年；Rasul 等人，2023 年）证明了大规模预训练对于信号预测的实用性，而后续的模型系列则强调了学习跨不同领域的共同光谱特性的实用性（Ansari 等人，2024 年；Das 等人，2023 年；Goswami 等人，2024 年）。大语言模型 (LLM) 用于时间序列分析的惊人能力进一步强调了跨领域的大规模广义预训练的实用性（Liu 等人，2023；Merrill 等人，2024；Thukral 等人，2025）。Foundation models, a recent advancement in machine learning, promise to address these limitations and accelerate progress in the field. These models often leverage self-supervised learning (SSL) over large-scale, heterogeneous, unlabeled datasets to learn universal representations which can generalize to a broad array of tasks. In the domain of health, these methods have successfully enabled improved performance on diverse applications, including radiology (Wu et al., 2025), pathology (Xu et al., 2024), and medical reasoning (McDuff et al., 2025b; Sellergren et al., 2025). Adjacently, early time-series foundation models (Garza et al., 2023; Rasul et al., 2023) demonstrated the utility of large-scale pretraining for signal forecasting, while subsequent families of models have emphasized the utility of learning common spectral properties across disparate domains (Ansari et al., 2024; Das et al., 2023; Goswami et al., 2024). The surprising capability of large-language models (LLMs) for time-series analysis further emphasizes the utility of scaled generalized pretraining across domains (Liu et al., 2023; Merrill et al., 2024; Thukral et al., 2025).

在可穿戴领域，最近的努力已经证明了使用 SSL 从大型多模态传感器数据中学习鲁棒表示的潜力（Abbaspourazad 等人，2024 年；Thapa 等人，2024 年；Yuan 等人，2024 年）。一项关键的进步是最近建立了可穿戴数据的缩放法则，并引入了能够直接从不完整的传感器信号中学习的架构（Narayanswamy 等人，2025；Xu 等人，2025）。然而，尽管取得了这些进展，但仍不清楚可穿戴数据的大规模预训练在多大程度上等同于对各种健康结果和见解的预测性能的有意义的改进。此外，当前的方法仍然受到两个关键瓶颈的限制：预训练数据的有限规模以及使单个通用嵌入适应许多不同的健康端点所需的大量手动工程。In the wearable domain, recent efforts have demonstrated the potential to learn robust representations from large corpora of multimodal sensor data employing SSL (Abbaspourazad et al., 2024; Thapa et al., 2024; Yuan et al., 2024). A pivotal advancement has been the recent establishment of scaling laws for wearable data and the introduction of architectures capable of learning directly from incomplete sensor signals (Narayanswamy et al., 2025; Xu et al., 2025). Yet, despite this progress, it remains unclear the full extent to which scaled pretraining on wearable data equates to meaningful improvements in predictive performance for diverse health outcomes and insights. Furthermore, current approaches remain constrained by two critical bottlenecks: the finite scale of pretraining data and the extensive manual engineering required to adapt a single generalist embedding to many distinct health endpoints.

在这项工作中，我们的目标是证明在预训练期间利用大量未标记的连续可穿戴传感器数据流可以导致预训练和下游任务目标的可预测改进，并且所得表示可以有效地适应许多结果。在此基础上，我们的目标是证明向代理健康教练提供这样的模型作为推理引擎比让教练直接处理可穿戴数据更有效。In this work we aim to prove that leveraging large, unlabeled streams of continuous wearable sensor data during pretraining can lead to predictable improvements in both pretraining and downstream task objectives, and that resulting representations can be efficiently adapted to many outcomes. Building on this, we aim to demonstrate that providing such a model as an inference engine to an agentic health coach is more effective than having the coach process only the wearable data directly.

为此，我们引入了 SensorFM（图 1），这是一个大型传感器To that end we introduce SensorFM (Fig. 1), a Large传感器Sensor可穿戴时间序列表示学习的基础模型，表现出跨健康领域的普遍性。通过将预训练扩展到前所未有的超过一万亿分钟的语料库（1foundation Model for wearable time-series representation learning which exhibits generalizability across health domains. By scaling pretraining to an unprecedented corpus of over one trillion minutes (1, 000 , 000 , 000 ,000）的传感器数据来自 500 万参与者（000) of sensor data drawn from five million participants (𝑁 = 5 , 000 ,000）和五种传感器模式，我们实现了感知人类生理学的高度适应性、通用性表示。据我们所知，这是迄今为止使用的最大、最多样化的可穿戴数据集（Erturk 等人，2025；Narayanswamy 等人，2025；Yuan 等人，2024）。我们通过一系列全面的下游健康任务评估 SensorFM，这些任务涵盖心血管健康、代谢风险、睡眠障碍、心理健康、生活方式选择和生理相关人口统计数据。我们使用源自受控临床和实验室研究的严格表型数据集来验证我们的模型（000) and five sensor modalities, we approach a highly adaptable, universal representation of sensed human physiology. To our knowledge, this is the largest and most diverse wearable dataset utilized to date (Erturk et al., 2025; Narayanswamy et al., 2025; Yuan et al., 2024). We evaluate SensorFM across a comprehensive suite of downstream health tasks that span cardiovascular health, metabolic risk, sleep disorders, mental health, lifestyle choices and physiologically relevant demographics. We validate our model using rigorously phenotyped datasets derived from controlled clinical and laboratory studies (𝑁 = 13 ,985）。我们进一步探索 SensorFM 重建缺失数据的能力以及对日常健康指标估计的影响。我们通过严格的测试来全面描述模型的功能985). We further explore the capabilities of SensorFM in reconstructing missing data and the resultant implications for daily health metric estimation. 对其扩展性、标签效率和可解释性的评估。evaluations of its scaling, label-efficiency, and interpretability.

此外，为了提高各种健康应用的预测性能上限，我们利用自动化代理框架，其灵感来自现代自我改进的代码生成系统（Aygün 等人，2025 年；Novikov 等人，2025 年），以最佳方式使 SensorFM 嵌入适应各个下游任务（图 2）。传统上，使通用表示适应各种下游应用程序需要定制架构工程来训练嵌入的特定领域模型。相比之下，我们提供了一个代理“课堂”，LLM 代理在其中迭代生成、测试和完善代码，以使用这些嵌入作为起点开发下游任务的模型。我们演示了这样的系统如何能够对下游解决方案空间进行更具可扩展性和系统性的探索，并利用该方法自主地进行 30 多项Furthermore, to push the upper bound of predictive performance for diverse health applications, we leverage an automated agentic framework, inspired by modern self-improving code-generation systems (Aygün et al., 2025; Novikov et al., 2025), to optimally adapt the SensorFM embeddings to individual downstream tasks (Figure 2). Traditionally, adapting general representations to a variety of downstream applications has required bespoke architecture engineering to train domain-specific models on the embeddings. In contrast, we provide an agentic “classroom” where LLM agents iteratively generate, test, and refine the code to develop models on downstream tasks using these embeddings as a starting point. We demonstrate how such a system enables more scalable and systematic exploration of the downstream solution space and leveraging this method autonomously conduct over 30,000 个单独的实验。我们评估允许代理扮演机器学习工程师角色的好处并分析代理衍生的解决方案。000 individual experiments. We evaluate the benefit of allowing agents to play the role of machine learning engineers and analyze the agent-derived solutions.

最后，鉴于人工智能语言模型在消费者健康查询中的广泛采用（Breda 等人，2026；McDuff 等人，2025b；Sumner 等人，2025；Tu 等人，2025），我们通过将 SensorFM 作为工具集成到个人健康代理中来建立模型的端到端实用程序（Heydari 等人，2025），并评估其增强上下文感知生理信息传递的实用性对用户的洞察（图 3）。我们对使用可穿戴数据和 SensorFM 预测或黄金标准地面实况测量生成的健康摘要进行了 40 多个小时的临床评估，涉及 1,860 个个人评分。与语言模型直接处理可穿戴数据的基线相比，使用 SensorFM 作为工具可以提高响应的特异性，并使响应更加个性化、适合上下文且更安全。与智能体能够获得真实测量结果的情况相比，我们观察到没有统计上的劣势。Finally, given the significant adoption of AI language models for consumer health queries (Breda et al., 2026; McDuff et al., 2025b; Sumner et al., 2025; Tu et al., 2025), we establish the model’s endto-end utility by integrating SensorFM as tool into a Personal Health Agent (Heydari et al., 2025) and evaluate its utility to enhance the delivery of context-aware physiological insights to users (Figure 3). We conduct over 40 hours of clinical evaluations of health summaries generated with wearable data and either SensorFM predictions or gold-standard ground-truth measurements involving 1,860 individual ratings. When compared to a baseline in which the language model processes the wearable data directly, using SensorFM as a tool improves the specificity of the responses and makes them more personalized, contextually appropriate and safer. When compared to a condition in which the agent has access to ground-truth measurements we observe no statistical inferiority.

总之，我们的工作代表了迄今为止对预训练可穿戴传感器基础模型最全面的评估，展示了如何通过规模化预训练产生的灵活嵌入针对许多下游健康应用进行有效微调，进而可以用来在个人层面提供有价值的见解。In summary, our work represents the most comprehensive evaluation of pretrained wearable sensor foundation models to date, demonstrating how flexible embeddings, produced through scaled pretraining, can be efficiently fine-tuned for many downstream health applications, which can in turn be leveraged to provide valuable insights at the person level.

2. 结果2. Results

我们首先从四个维度描述 SensorFM 的属性并评估其性能：资源扩展、来自多个前瞻性研究的判别性健康任务的预测性能、填充和预测传感器数据的生成能力，以及最终对潜在空间中模型嵌入的解释。然后，我们呈现代理搜索方法的结果，用于自动设计和完善特定于应用程序的预测“头”，并跨分类和基于回归的健康任务评估该系统。最后，我们评估 SensorFM 作为个人健康代理的工具，并招募一组临床医生来评估该模型的预测在为一组 31 个真实（即非合成）健康档案创建健康摘要时的效用。We first characterize the properties and evaluate the performance of SensorFM along four dimensions: resource scaling, predictive performance across discriminative health tasks sourced from multiple prospective studies, generative capabilities for infilling and forecasting sensor data, and finally interpretation of the model embeddings in a latent space. We then present results from an agentic search method used to automatically design and refine application-specific prediction “heads” and evaluate this system across classification and regression-based health tasks. Finally we evaluate SensorFM as a tool for a Personal Health Agent and recruit a cohort of clinicians to evaluate the utility of the model’s predictions when creating health summaries for a set of 31 real (i.e., non-synthetic) health profiles.

2.1.扩展可穿戴健康的通用模型2.1. Scaling a Generalist Model for Wearable Health

缩放法则的建立以及基础模型在语言和视觉等领域的成功表明，模型性能通常不是由架构设计驱动，而是由计算、模型容量以及最终的训练数据量驱动（Kaplan 等人，2020 年；Zhai 等人，2022 年）。扩展定律提供了经验证据，可用于帮助预测如果增加任何（或全部）这些资源，可能实现的性能增益。时间序列和可穿戴传感器信号的缩放的初步经验证据已被记录The establishment of scaling laws, and the resultant success of foundation models in domains such as language and vision, has shown that model performance is often driven not by architectural design, but rather by compute, model capacity, and ultimately the volume of training data (Kaplan et al., 2020; Zhai et al., 2022). Scaling laws provide empirical evidence that can be used to help anticipate the performance gains that could potentially be achieved if any (or all) of these resources are increased. Initial empirical evidence of scaling has been documented for time-series and wearable sensor signals

（Narayanswamy 等人，2025；Shi 等人，2024；Zhang 等人，2025）。然而，一套针对可穿戴传感器数据的系统性扩展实验仍然缺乏，这些实验表明渐进式预训练收益可以预见地转化为在估计有意义的健康结果方面的可测量的改进。(Narayanswamy et al., 2025; Shi et al., 2024; Zhang et al., 2025). Yet, a systematic set of scaling experiments on wearable sensor data demonstrating that progressive pretraining gains predictably translate to measurable improvements in estimating meaningful health outcomes is still absent.

在这项工作中，我们比以前的工作大幅增加了预训练和计算的规模。具体来说，我们扩大规模In this work we substantially increase the scale of pretraining and compute beyond previous efforts. Specifically, we scale over四个数量级four orders of magnitude预训练数据量（200 万到 20 亿小时的多模态传感器数据）和模型大小（100K 到 100M 参数）。我们的最大数据量是数据小时数增加了 50 倍，超过了之前针对分钟分辨率数据训练的模型所使用的 4000 万小时（Narayanswamy 等人，2025 年）。与用于在小时分辨率数据上训练模型的 25 亿小时相比（Erturk 等人，2025），我们的更高for both pretraining data volume (two million to two billion hours of multimodal sensor data) and model size (100K to 100M parameters). Our maximum data volume is a 50x increase in data-hours over and above the 40 million hours used by prior work for models trained on minute-resolution data (Narayanswamy et al., 2025). Compared to the 2.5 billion hours used to train models on hour-resolution data (Erturk et al., 2025), our higher细致地minutely分辨率数据使总数据量增加了 50 倍。在我们的分析过程中，我们通过采样的个体数量或相关的多模式数据小时来引用数据量：resolution data accounts for a 50x increase in total data volume. Throughout our analysis we refer to data volumes by the number of individuals sampled or the associated multimodal data hours:5K（2 × 10[6] 小时），(2 × 10[6] hrs.),50K（2 × 10[7] 小时），(2 × 10[7] hrs.),50万500K（2 × 10[8] 小时），(2 × 10[8] hrs.),5M（2 × 10[9] 小时）。我们根据 SensorFM 型号的容量来引用它们：(2 × 10[9] hrs.). We evaluate the effect of this scaled pretraining on classification, regression, and generative tasks.

扩展预训练数据和模型容量的重要性。The Importance of Scaling Pretraining Data and Model Capacity.我们观察到，预训练验证损失随着数据量和模型容量的增加而成反比（表 ED.5）。在此过程中，我们验证了扩展这些资源会带来模型性能的可预测改进，并且在判别性和生成性下游任务中都观察到类似的改进。例如，当使用最大 5M 受试者数据量进行预训练时，SensorFM-B 始终优于较小的 SensorFM-XXS。缩放模型在重建预训练任务上实现了 31% 的验证损失 (MSE) 降低，在生成任务中实现了 28% 的损失 (平均 MSE) 降低。在判别任务上，缩放模型实现了 Δ 的平均改进We observe that the pretraining validation loss inversely scales with increases in data volume and model capacity (Table ED.5). In so doing, we verify that scaling these resources leads to predictable improvements in model performance and that similar improvements are observed for both discriminative and generative downstream tasks. For example, when pretrained with the largest 5M subject data volume, SensorFM-B consistently outperforms the smaller SensorFM-XXS. The scaled model achieves a 31% reduced validation loss (MSE) on the reconstruction pretraining task, and a 28% reduced loss (avg. MSE) across generative tasks. On discriminative tasks, the scaled model achieves a mean improvement of Δ𝐴𝑈𝐶 = 0 .09 关于分类任务和 Δ09 on classification tasks and Δ𝑟 = 0 .21关于回归任务。21 on regression tasks.

至关重要的是，我们观察到最显着的收益是通过联合扩展来实现的Crucially, we observe that the most significant gains are achieved through the joint scaling of两者both数据量和模型容量。数据和容量按数量级按比例缩放，导致生成预训练和判别训练后性能的近线性改进（见图 1e）。data volume and model capacity. The proportional scaling of data and capacity by orders of magnitude, leads to near-linear improvements in both generative pretraining and discriminative post-training performance (see Figure 1e).在这一发现的推动下，除非明确说明，否则所有以下结果均假设模型是使用按其容量按比例缩放的数据量进行训练的Driven by this finding, all following results, unless explicitly stated, assume that models are trained with data volumes proportionally scaled to their capacity。这种联合缩放对歧视性健康任务的影响在图 4 中得到了进一步可视化，并在表 ED.6 中呈现，其中在各个模型变体中，SensorFM-B 的任务获胜率为 33/35，而 XXS 预计在 33/35 的任务中排名最后。. The impact of this joint scaling on discriminative health tasks is further visualized in Figure 4 and presented in Tables ED.6 where across model variants, SensorFM-B boasts a task win-rate of 33/35, while XXS expectedly ranks last on 33/35 tasks.

2.2.学习跨健康领域有用的表示2.2. Learning a Representation Useful Across Health Domains

我们评估了来自多项前瞻性研究的 35 项有区别的健康任务中的 SensorFM 学习嵌入（参见方法 M.2.2）。这些任务跨越We evaluate the SensorFM-learned embeddings across a diverse range of 35 discriminative health tasks derived from multiple prospective studies (see Methods M.2.2). These tasks span心血管健康Cardiovascular Health (6), 代谢健康Metabolic Health (8), 心理健康Mental Health (8), 睡眠Sleep (3), 人口统计Demographic(4) 和(4), and生活方式因素Lifestyle Factors(6)，完整的任务列表见表 ED.3。为了询问预训练嵌入的质量，我们利用(6), with the full list of tasks found in Table ED.3. In order to interrogate the quality of the pretrained embeddings, we leverage a冷冻的frozenSensorFM 编码器并学习计算高效的线性头，以使嵌入适应各个应用。考虑到带注释的示例数量有限，这些头经过训练，嵌入减少到 50 个主成分。我们将 SensorFM 与使用源自可穿戴传感器流的工程特征进行训练的监督模型进行基线对比（参见方法 M.3.6）。我们通过训练具有和不具有人口统计特征的模型，与仅使用人口统计特征训练的基线监督模型进行比较，进一步评估所学习的传感器表示的提升。SensorFM encoder and learn a computationally efficient linear head to adapt the embeddings to individual applications. To account for the limited number of annotated examples, these heads are trained with embeddings reduced to 50 principal components. We baseline SensorFM against supervised models trained with engineered features derived from the wearable sensor streams (see Methods M.3.6). We further assess the lift of the learned sensor representation, by training models both with and without demographic features, comparing against baseline supervised models trained only with demographic features.

学到的表征可以推广到不同的健康结果。The Learned Representation Generalizes to Diverse Health Outcomes.我们发现森We find that Sen-

7

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

sorFM 通过大规模预训练，学习了一种能够成功推广到广泛健康结果的表示方法。如表 ED.9 所示，在 SensorFM 学习嵌入之上训练的线性头始终优于使用工程特征训练的监督基线。具体来说，SensorFM 在 35 项判别任务中的 34 项上优于该监督基线（图 1.d）。 SensorFM 在 30 项判别性任务中的 24 项上优于仅针对人口特征进行训练的基线。sorFM, through scaled pretraining, learns a representation capable of successfully generalizing to a broad range of health outcomes. As illustrated in Table ED.9, linear heads trained on-top of the SensorFM learned embeddings consistently outperform supervised baselines trained with engineered features. Specifically, SensorFM outperforms this supervised baseline on 34 of 35 discriminative tasks (Figure 1.d). SensorFM outperforms a baseline trained on only demographic features on 24 of 30 discriminative tasks.

人口统计特征的效用。The Utility of Demographic Features.正如表 ED.9 中所强调的，我们发现 SensorFM 的预测能力通常会通过添加人口统计特征（30 个判别任务中的 22 个）而得到增强，尽管幅度不大。虽然人口统计特征往往会提高 SensorFM 的性能，但有趣的是，我们发现 SensorFM 对人口统计特征的依赖性随着规模的扩大而降低。与 35 项任务中的 33 项的较小模型变体和监督基线相比，SensorFM-B 从添加的人口统计特征中获得的收益较小，这意味着这些生理相关特征可以通过大规模预训练隐式学习（表 ED.8）。当适应歧视性下游任务以及人口统计特征时，嵌入的特征重要性也观察到了类似的趋势。大规模预训练的模型提供了更稳健的表示，从而减少了对人口先验的依赖（图 ED.9.b）。As highlighted in Table ED.9, we find that the predictive power of SensorFM is often, though modestly, enhanced through the addition of demographic features (22 of 30 discriminative tasks). While demographic features tend to improve the performance of SensorFM, interestingly we find that the dependence of SensorFM on demographic features decreases with scale. SensorFM-B realizes smaller gains from added demographic features as compared to both smaller model variants and supervised baselines on 33 of 35 tasks, implying that these physiologically relevant traits may be implicitly learned through pretraining at scale (Table ED.8). A similar trend is observed in the feature importance attributed to embeddings when adapted to discriminative downstream tasks alongside demographic features. Models pretrained at scale provide a more robust representation which reduces the reliance on demographic priors (Figure ED.9.b).

此外，对于某些任务（例如心血管 Dx、胰岛素抵抗、ASCVD 风险、Framingham 风险等），我们发现仅使用人口统计数据训练的模型即可提供显着的预测能力（图 4）。对于此类任务，SensorFM 可能仅在极端的预训练规模上优于这些人口统计基线（例如，SensorFM-B 经过 5000 万周的数据训练）。对于这些任务的子集（ASCVD 风险、Framingham 风险、Framingham 30 风险），仅人口统计模型的强劲表现可能源于明确使用人口统计特征来计算这些风险评分。Additionally, for some tasks (e.g., cardiovascular Dx, insulin resistance, ASCVD risk, Framingham risk, and more), we find that models trained with demographics alone provide significant predictive power, (Figure 4). For such tasks, SensorFM may only outperform these demographic baselines at extreme pretraining scales (e.g., SensorFM-B trained on 50M weeks of data). For a subset of these tasks (ASCVD Risk, Framingham Risk, Framingham 30 Risk), the strong performance of demographic-only models likely stems from the explicit use of demographic features to calculate these risk scores.

大规模预训练可实现标签高效适应。Scaled Pretraining Enables Label Efficient Adaptation.我们发现，与监督基线相比，规模化预训练和由此产生的 SensorFM 学习表示可以提高标签效率。如图 ED.5 所示，我们通过使用不同百分比的下游训练量训练模型来测试这一点。由于标记样本很少，人口统计先验可以作为许多任务的强有力的预测因子。然而，随着标记样本数量的增加，SensorFM 很快就超越了仅人口统计的基线，并始终优于特征工程基线，较大的模型变体 (B) 优于较小的变体 (XXS)。We find that scaled pretraining and the resultant learned representation of SensorFM enables improved label efficiency compared to supervised baselines. As depicted in Figure ED.5 we tested this by training models with varying percentages of downstream training volumes. With very few labeled samples, demographic priors act as a strong predictor for many tasks. However, as the number of labeled samples increases, SensorFM soon outperforms demographic-only baselines and consistently outperforms the feature engineered baselines, with larger model variants (B) outperforming smaller variants (XXS).

2.3.利用学到的生成能力2.3. Exploiting the Learned Generative Capabilities

SensorFM 利用重构前置文本任务，可以直接从未标记的、被动收集的传感器数据中进行学习。利用类似 MAE 的架构（He et al., 2022; Xu et al., 2025），我们的方法可以原生处理可穿戴传感器流中固有的缺失，这是不同传感器配置、操作模式和用户行为的结果。在预训练过程中，SensorFM 学习了一个能够重建消融观测值的解码器，这反过来又转化为数据插补和信号预测等生成能力。图 ED.6 展示了从我们的预训练参与者那里获得的全天窗口示例SensorFM leverages a reconstructive pre-text task which enables learning directly from unlabeled, passively collected sensor data. Leveraging an MAE-like architecture (He et al., 2022; Xu et al., 2025), our method natively handles the missingness inherent in wearable sensor streams, a consequence of varying sensor configurations, operating modes, and user behaviors. During pretraining, SensorFM learns a decoder capable of reconstructing ablated observations, which in-turn translates to generative capacities such as data imputation and signal forecasting. Figure ED.6 presents examples of day-long windows obtained from participants in our pretraining验证validation设置，将原始模型输入与重建输出进行比较。图 ED.7 和 ED.8 描绘了单个传感器特征填充的线图，并突出显示了 SensorFM 重建中的非线性动态。set, comparing the original model input to the reconstructed output. Figures ED.7 and ED.8 depict line graphs for individual sensor feature infilling and highlight the non-linear dynamics within the SensorFM reconstructions.

SensorFM 学习填充和预测传感器数据。SensorFM Learns to Fill and Forecast Sensor Data.SensorFM 通过其生成预训练，学会成功地插补、插值和推断缺失或未观察到的数据（参见表 ED.10）。具体来说，我们发现 SensorFM 在随机插补方面优于性能最佳基线 74.8%，在时间插值方面优于最佳基线 38.8%，在时间外推方面优于最佳基线 39.6%，在传感器信号插补方面优于最佳基线 83.7%。SensorFM, through its generative pretraining, learns to successfully impute, interpolate, and extrapolate missing or unobserved data (see Table ED.10). Specifically, we find that SensorFM outperforms the best-performing baselines by 74.8% on random imputation, 38.8% on temporal interpolation, 39.6% on temporal extrapolation, and 83.7% on sensor signal imputation.

由于各种原因。此类中断可能会严重影响个人的健康摘要统计数据。因此，为个人提供对其汇总统计数据的更准确的估计可能是有利的，从而使他们能够更好地评估他们的整体健康状况。为此，我们探索利用 SensorFM 的生成功能来估算缺失数据的潜力，以便更实际地估计一个人的日常指标。具体来说，SensorFM 利用时间插值来填充缺失的片段。正如表 ED.11 中强调的那样，我们发现在存在缺失或消融数据的情况下，SensorFM 能够生成更可靠的日常指标。具体来说，我们表明，当消除一天中连续 60 分钟的数据时，SensorFM 在每日步数预测中保留了 99.7% 的准确度，在深度睡眠预测中保留了 99.9% 的准确度，在轻度运动跟踪中保留了 99.2% 的准确度，从而减轻了基线表现中观察到的低估情况（图 1.c）。for a variety of reasons. Such interruptions can significantly skew an individual’s health summary statistics. As such, it may be advantageous to provide individuals with more accurate estimates of their summary statistics allowing them to better gauge their overall health status. Towards this end, we explore the potential of leveraging SensorFM’s generative capabilities to impute missing data in order to more realistically estimate a person’s daily metrics. Specifically, SensorFM leverages temporal interpolation to infill missing segments. As highlighted in Table ED.11, we find that in the presence of missing or ablated data, SensorFM is able to produce more reliable daily metrics. Specifically, we show that when ablating 60 contiguous minutes of data in a day, SensorFM retains 99.7% accuracy in daily step count prediction, 99.9% accuracy in deep sleep prediction, and 99.2% accuracy in light exercise tracking, mitigating the underestimation observed in baseline performance (Figure 1.c).

2.4.理解和量化学习潜在空间2.4. Understand and Quantifying the Learning Latent Space

对潜在空间中 SensorFM 嵌入的分析和可视化为学习的表示、其结构及其在下游任务中的应用提供了宝贵的见解。为此，我们预测并可视化了许多健康结果的潜在空间，分析了跨尺度模型的嵌入距离和内在维度，并解释了嵌入对于下游健康结果的重要性。Analysis and visualization of the SensorFM embeddings in latent space provide valuable insight into the learned representation, its structure, and its application to downstream tasks. Towards this end, we project and visualize the latent space for a number of health outcomes, analyze the embedding distances and intrinsic dimensionality of the model across scales, and interpret the importance of the embeddings with respect to downstream health outcomes.

使用任务标签可视化嵌入。Visualizing Embeddings with Task Labels.我们使用统一流形逼近和投影 (UMAP)（McInnes 等人，2018）将高维潜在嵌入向量从最大模型减少到二维（图 ED.10）。该模型的流形可以在没有明确标签的情况下深入了解预训练期间学到的信息。我们用来自不同下游数据集的标签直观地注释这些点。请注意，虽然所有图都使用相同的 UMAP 投影，但并非所有参与者/日期都具有所有任务的标签。嵌入中捕获了 BMI、年龄和性别的明显人口变化模式。最终，这些可视化强调了这种规模的自监督预训练如何自然地将碎片传感器流组织成具有生理意义的拓扑，从而验证基础模型方法作为感知人类健康的通用表示。We use Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) (McInnes et al., 2018) to reduce the high-dimensional latent embedding vectors from our largest model down to two dimensions (Figure ED.10). The manifold of the model provides insight into the information learned during pretraining in the absence of explicit labels. We visually annotate these points with labels from different downstream datasets. Note that although all plots use the same UMAP projection, not all participants/days have labels for all tasks. There are clear patterns of demographic shifts for BMI, Age, and Gender captured within the embedding. Ultimately, these visualizations underscore how self-supervised pretraining at this scale naturally organizes fragmented sensor streams into a physiologically meaningful topology, validating a foundation model approach as a universal representation of sensed human health.

嵌入距离。Embedding Distances.为了研究模型规模如何影响潜在空间密度，我们比较了不同模型大小的用户表示的分散性（图 ED.11.a），这是一种量化参与者相似性的方法，如之前的工作所示（Kiyasseh 等人，2021）。我们发现，虽然所有模型都产生单峰、右偏距离分布，但它们的潜在空间色散差异很大。 SensorFM-S 模型学习最紧密的聚类表示，而 SensorFM-B 模型产生最广泛的嵌入分布。最小的模型 (SensorFM-XXS) 也表现出类似的广泛分布，表明最佳模型容量和数据量在塑造表示密度方面存在相互作用。潜在空间的这种结构演变证实，数据和参数的联合缩放对于产生足够表达的嵌入空间以捕获跨不同临床领域的细微差别、受试者间差异至关重要。To investigate how model scale influences latent space density, we compare the dispersion of user representations across different model sizes (Figure ED.11.a), an approach to quantifying participant similarity as seen in previous works (Kiyasseh et al., 2021). We find that while all models yield unimodal, right-skewed distance distributions, their latent space dispersion varies significantly. The SensorFM-S model learns the most tightly clustered representations, whereas the SensorFM-B model produces the broadest embedding spread. The smallest model (SensorFM-XXS) also exhibits a similarly broad spread, pointing to an interplay between optimal model capacity and data volume in shaping representation density. This structural evolution of the latent space confirms that the joint scaling of data and parameters is crucial for yielding an embedding space expressive enough to capture the nuanced, inter-subject variations across diverse clinical domains.

固有维数和可压缩性Intrinsic Dimensionality and Compressibility。我们评估了跨模型尺度的嵌入的可压缩性和信息结构（图 ED.11.b）。分析表明模型嵌入具有高度可压缩性；特别是对于较大的模型，表示可以减少到 150-200 维，而不会显着损失方差。此外，不同模型大小的方差缩放行为存在显着差异。最小模型 (XXS) 捕获其前 20 个主成分内大约 90% 的方差，然后呈平线，表明维度崩溃——过度依赖受限特征流形。相反，最大模型B表现出很强的各向异性。它在其主导方向上学习了一个大的“超级特征”，仅第一个主成分就解释了大约 40% 的总方差。在这个初始尖峰之后，曲线变平，表明虽然大型模型严重依赖于主要的主要成分，但它. We evaluate the compressibility and informational structure of the embeddings across model scales (Figure ED.11.b). An analysis reveals that the model embeddings are highly compressible; particularly for the larger models, representations can be reduced to 150-200 dimensions without significant loss of variance. Furthermore, the variance scaling behaviours differ significantly across model sizes. The smallest model (XXS) captures approximately 90% of the variance within its first 20 principal components and then flatlines, indicating dimensional collapse—an over-reliance on a restricted feature manifold. Conversely, the largest model B shows strong anisotropy. It learns a large "super-feature" in its dominant direction, with the first principal component alone explaining approximately 40% of the total variance. After this initial spike, the curve flattens out, indicating that while the large model relies heavily on a dominant primary component, it

10

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

重要的是保留了分布在更高维度上的细微生理信息的重要“长尾”。因此，大规模传感器模型提供了强大的双重优势：它们将大量连续的可穿戴数据流提炼成高效、可压缩的接口以进行下游建模，同时至关重要地保留了预测情绪和睡眠障碍等异构结果所必需的微妙的长尾信号。importantly preserves a significant "long tail" of nuanced physiological information distributed across higher dimensions. Consequently, large-scale sensor models provide a powerful dual advantage: they distill large, continuous streams of wearable data into highly efficient, compressible interfaces for downstream modelling, while crucially preserving the subtle, long-tail signals essential for predicting heterogeneous outcomes such as mood and sleep disorders.

解释嵌入的重要性。Interpreting Embedding Importances.为了更好地理解潜在嵌入向量的结构，我们利用 SHapley 加法解释（SHAP）（Lundberg 和 Lee，2017）来评估各个潜在维度的影响。我们计算模型上每个线性预测头的 SHAP 值，然后计算成对的In order to better understand the structure of the latent embedding vector, we leverage SHapley Additive explanations (SHAP) (Lundberg and Lee, 2017) to assess the effect of individual latent dimensions. We calculate SHAP values for each linear prediction head on the model and then compute the pair-wise𝑐𝑜𝑠𝑖𝑛𝑒每对任务的标准化、精确 SHAP 归因（权重崩溃分析）概况之间的相似性。这表明不同任务利用基础模型中相同的底层嵌入维度的程度（图 ED.9.a）。通过高度相关的标签（例如 ASCVD 风险和 Framingham 风险以及体重和 BMI）观察到嵌入的预期相似性。然而，我们观察到其他任务之间的联系，包括睡眠障碍和 PSS 评分，以及 HOMA-IR 和 PHQ-8 评分。similarity between the normalized, exact SHAP attribution (weight collapse analysis) profiles of each pair of tasks. This indicates the degree to which distinct tasks leverage the same underlying embedding dimensions from the foundational model (Figure ED.9.a). Expected similarities in embeddings are observed with highly correlated labels such as ASCVD risk and Framingham risk, and weight and BMI. However, we observe links between other tasks, including sleep impairment and PSS score, and HOMA-IR and PHQ-8 score.

2.5.代理驱动的下游模型头搜索2.5. Agent Driven Search of Downstream Model Heads

通用的预训练模型嵌入可以推广到许多预测任务，因此很有吸引力，因为它们可以更轻松地构建特定于应用程序的模型，尤其是在标签稀疏的情况下。因此，有效设计新预测“头”的方法使这些嵌入能够更快地适应新任务。有效地适应下游预测任务的嵌入的瓶颈通常是特征工程、模型架构选择和超参数调整所需的专业知识和迭代工作。在健康领域尤其如此，其中观测数据通常受到重大限制（例如稀疏性、噪声、有限数量和标签不平衡）。Versatile pretrained model embeddings which generalize to many predictive tasks are attractive as they enable application-specific models to be built more easily, especially when labels are sparse. As such, methods which efficiently design new prediction “heads” allow these embeddings to be more rapidly adapted to novel tasks. A bottleneck to effectively adapting embeddings for downstream predictive tasks is often the expertise and iterative work required for feature engineering, model architecture selection, and hyperparameter tuning. This is especially true in health domains, where observational data is typically subject to significant constraints (e.g., sparsity, noise, limited volume, and imbalance of labels).

我们实现了一个混合建模系统，改编自 Aygün 等人。 （2025），利用语言模型的推理和代码编写能力以及迭代解决方案搜索框架来自动适应新领域的通用嵌入。具体来说，我们利用“课堂”配置（M.5 中描述），这是一组自我演化的算法生成步骤，其中解决方案合成被制定为由并行 LLM 代理解决的竞争性协作优化问题（图 2）。利用这个框架，我们快速迭代了 30 多个We implement a hybrid modeling system, adapted from Aygün et al. (2025), to leverage the reasoning and code-writing abilities of language models alongside an iterative-solution-search framework to autonomously adapt general embeddings to new domains. Specifically, we leverage a "classroom" configuration (described in M.5), a set of self-evolving algorithm generation steps in which the solution synthesis is formulated as a competitive, collaborative optimisation problem solved by parallel LLM agents (Figure 2). Leveraging this framework we rapidly iterate over 30,000 个代理提出的解决方案。000 agent-proposed solutions.

健康任务方面的改进。Improvements Across Health Tasks.我们发现，与应用于 SensorFM 嵌入的简单线性头相比，这种代理方法可以提高各种判别性健康任务的性能。具体来说，源自课堂的代理解决方案在 20 个分类任务中的 16 个上取得了改进的性能，在 15 个回归任务中的 12 个上具有更大的皮尔逊相关性（图 5 和表 ED.12）。请注意，我们报告这些分类结果的 F1，因为许多解决方案都是集成方法，无法获得用于计算 ROC 曲线的连续输出。通过这样做，我们展示了人工智能代理充当机器学习科学家的潜力，减少了传统上与将通用嵌入适应多个端点相关的工程负担。We find that this agentic approach leads to improved performance as compared to a simple linear head applied to the SensorFM embeddings across a breadth of discriminative health tasks. Specifically, classroom-derived agent solutions boast improved performance on 16 of 20 classification tasks and greater Pearson correlations on 12 of 15 regression tasks (Figure 5 and Table ED.12). Note that we report F1 for these classification results as many solutions were ensemble methods from which it is not possible to obtain a continuous output with which to compute an ROC curve. In so doing we demonstrate the potential of AI agents to act as machine learning scientists, reducing the engineering burden traditionally associated with adapting general embeddings to multiple endpoints.

解决方案性能可随着法学硕士的能力而扩展。Solution Performance Scales with LLM Capabilities.对 LLM 模型变体组织的代理解决方案的分析揭示了一个有趣的模式，最近的模型表现出更强的性能（见图 ED.12.a）。当这些模型用常用的人工智能分析智能指数[1]来表征时，智能指数越高的模型平均提供更好的解决方案。此外，我们发现“学生”（代理）之间的协作事件，触发Analyses of the agent solutions organized by LLM model variant reveals an interesting pattern, with more recent models exhibiting stronger performance (see Figure ED.12.a). When these models are characterized by the commonly used Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index[1] , models with higher intelligence indexes provide better solutions on average. Furthermore, we find that collaboration events between “students” (agents), triggered

1https://artificialanalysis.ai/evaluations/artificial-analysis-intelligence-index

11

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

当给定代理表现出稳定的性能并允许反思其自己的解决方案或其他代理的解决方案时，可以启用不太智能的模型来缩小这种性能差距。然而，最佳性能通常是从 Gemini 的较新版本中观察到的（Comanici 等人，2025）。when a given agent demonstrates plateauing performance and is allowed to reflect on its own solutions or the solutions of other agents, enables less intelligent models to close this performance gap. However, the best performance is typically observed from more recent versions of Gemini (Comanici et al., 2025).

分析找到的解决方案。Analysis of the Found Solutions.对通过课堂算法搜索找到的最佳解决方案进行元分析（参见图 ED.12.b）表明，几乎所有得分最高的解决方案都将嵌入特征空间的维度减少到 50 – 100 维之间，最有可能减少输入空间的方差以匹配标记示例的稀缺性。结果还表明，线性模型比非线性模型更常见。不到四分之一的最佳解决方案采用了集成。虽然手动搜索这个解决方案空间很容易处理，但它非常耗时且通常效率低下，随着下游预测任务数量的增加，变得越来越不可行。相比之下，这种代理驱动的方法可以实现高效迭代，最终解决方案的平均质量随着时间的推移单调提高（图 2.d）。Meta analysis of the best solutions found through the classroom algorithm search (see Figure ED.12.b) reveals that almost all top-scoring solutions reduced the dimensionality of the embedding feature space to between 50 − 100 dimensions, most likely to reduce the variance of the input space to match the scarcity of labeled examples. The results additionally reveal that linear models were more common than non-linear models. Ensembles were employed in just under a quarter of the best solutions. While manually searching this space of solutions is tractable, it is time consuming and often inefficient, becoming increasingly less feasible as the number of downstream prediction tasks increases. By contrast, this agent driven approach allows for efficient iteration, with the average quality of final solutions improving monotonically over time (Figure 2.d).

2.6. SensorFM 作为工具的代理使用2.6. Agentic Use of the SensorFM as a Tool

一个关键的悬而未决的问题是，与仅限于手工功能的代理推理相比，提供适用于多个端点的 SensorFM 作为个人健康代理的推理引擎是否可以改善对用户健康查询的响应。为此，我们在三种不同的实验条件下评估健康代理的性能：A critical open question is whether providing SensorFM, adapted to multiple endpoints, as an inference engine to a personal health agent, yields improvements in the responses to user health queries as compared to the agent reasoning limited to handcrafted features. Towards this end, We evaluate the performance of a health agent under three distinct experimental conditions:

  • 条件（A）：Condition (A): 人口统计数据 + 每日可穿戴指标 + SensorFM 预测Demographics + Daily Wearable Metrics + SensorFM PredictionsAgent 接收人口统计数据、特征设计的每日指标和 SensorFM 预测（例如，预测的高脂血症状态、预测的高血压状态等）Agent receives demographics, feature-engineered daily metrics, and SensorFM predictions (e.g., predicted hyperlipidemia state, predicted hypertension state, etc.)

  • 条件（B）：Condition (B): 人口统计数据 + 每日可穿戴指标 + 基本事实Demographics + Daily Wearable Metrics + Ground Truth。与条件 (A) 类似，但用 SensorFM 预测替换为患者的实际地面实况目标（例如，实际高脂血症状态、实际高血压状态等）。. Similar to Condition (A) but with SensorFM predictions replaced by the patient’s actual ground-truth targets (e.g., actual hyperlipidemia state, actual hypertension state, etc.).

  • 条件（C）：Condition (C): 人口统计数据 + 每日可穿戴指标Demographics + Daily Wearable Metrics基线比较器：代理仅接收人口统计数据和特征设计的每日指标。Baseline comparator: agent receives only demographics and feature-engineered daily metrics.

三、讨论3. Discussion

3.1.学习感知人类生理学的缩放表示3.1. Learning a Scaled Representation of Sensed Human Physiology

我们的扩展实验揭示了模型性能、模型容量和预训练数据量之间的可预测关系。当模型大小和数据量同时增加时，观察到最一致的性能提升，图 1.e 表明我们的预训练方法尚未饱和。这强化了这样的假设：这两个维度对于通过自我监督预训练最大化收益和学习感知生理学的稳健表示至关重要。总的来说，我们的研究结果证明了如何通过利用广泛、多样化、高保真的数据集来推动可穿戴人工智能的进步。与受益于网络规模开放语料库的计算机视觉或自然语言处理不同，由于隐私和技术方面的考虑，可穿戴数据本质上难以聚合、标准化和共享。因此，数据保真度、新模式的添加和纵向尺度可能会成为传感器基础模型持续发展的主要比例因素。Our scaling experiments reveal a predictable relationship between model performance, model capacity, and pretraining data volume. The most consistent performance gains were observed when model size and data volume were increased concurrently, with Figure 1.e indicating that our pretraining method has yet to saturate. This reinforces the hypothesis that both dimensions are essential to maximize gains through self-supervised pretraining and to learn robust representations of sensed physiology. Overall, our findings demonstrate how advancements in wearable AI may be driven by leveraging expansive, diverse, high-fidelity datasets. Unlike computer vision or natural language processing, which benefit from web-scale open corpora, wearable data is inherently difficult to aggregate, standardize, and share due to privacy and technical considerations. Consequently, data fidelity, additions of new modalities and longitudinal scale will likely be a primary scaling factor for the continued evolution of sensor foundation models.

3.2.可推广嵌入在可穿戴健康领域的实用性3.2. The Utility of Generalizable Embeddings for Wearable Health

我们证明，对大量可穿戴传感器数据进行大规模自我监督预训练可以产生感知到的人类生理和行为的可靠表示，并有效地转移到不同的健康领域。在 35 项不同的健康和行为任务中，包括心血管健康、代谢功能、睡眠结构、心理健康、生活方式和人口因素，SensorFM 嵌入的线性探针始终优于经过工程特征训练的监督基线。至关重要的是，即使没有特定于任务的架构，SensorFM 也能实现强大的预测准确性，这表明，当经过足够规模的训练时，可穿戴基础模型可以学习广泛有用且标签高效的表示。这在医疗保健领域尤其重要，因为在这些领域，获得高质量、真实的标签通常价格昂贵且需要大量劳动力。We demonstrate that large-scale self-supervised pretraining on a large corpora of wearable sensor data produces a robust representation of sensed human physiology and behavior that transfers effectively to diverse health domains. Across 35 distinct health and behavioral tasks, encompassing cardiovascular health, metabolic function, sleep architecture, mental health, lifestyle and demographic factors, linear probes of the SensorFM embeddings consistently outperformed supervised baselines trained with engineered features. Crucially, SensorFM achieves robust predictive accuracy even without task-specific architectures, suggesting that when trained at sufficient scale, wearable foundation models can learn representations that are broadly useful and label-efficient. This is particularly critical across healthcare domains, where high-quality, ground-truth labels are often expensive and labor-intensive to obtain.

为此，我们的结果表明，规模化预训练对于涉及异质和弱表达表型的应用可能特别有价值，例如心理健康（例如抑郁/焦虑、PHQ-8 评分等）。心理健康状况涉及主观和客观指标的混合（Newson et al., 2020），呈现出不同的临床表现（Hwang et al., 2008；Kivimäki et al., 2020；McLean et al., 2011），并受到复杂的时间动态的控制（Nelson et al., 2017）。通过对日常生活中存在广泛变化的大型语料库进行预训练，SensorFM 可以更好地边缘化“令人讨厌的”变化，并保留在不同人群中普遍存在的潜在生理信息。然而，我们警告说，人口层面的模型与个人层面的预测不同。未来的研究应该采用纵向个性化模型来更好地评估人体内条件和健康状况随时间的变化。To this end, our results suggest that scaled pretraining may be particularly valuable for applications involving heterogeneous and weakly expressed phenotypes, such as mental health (e.g. Depression/Anxiety, PHQ-8 score, etc.). Mental health conditions involve a blend of subjective and objective indicators (Newson et al., 2020), present with diverse clinical manifestations (Hwang et al., 2008; Kivimäki et al., 2020; McLean et al., 2011), and are governed by complex temporal dynamics (Nelson et al., 2017). By pretraining on a large corpus with broad variation in daily routines, SensorFM may better marginalize “nuisance” variation and retain latent physiological information that generalizes across diverse populations. However, we caution that population-level models are distinct from individual-level forecasting; future research should employ longitudinal personalized modeling to better evaluate within-person changes in conditions and health over time.

3.3.加速模型开发3.3. Accelerating Model Development

灵活且富有表现力的预训练嵌入可以极大地提高我们开发新下游模型的效率。 SensorFM 的小样本性能说明了通过缩放预训练学习到的表示的通用性和高效适应性。然而，具有高质量标签的有限数据量仍然对下游任务构成瓶颈Flexible and expressive pretrained embeddings can dramatically increase how efficiently we can develop new downstream models. The few-shot performance of SensorFM speaks to the generalizability and efficient adaptability of the representation learned through scaled pretraining. However, the limited volume of data with high-quality labels continues to pose a bottleneck on downstream task

14

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

性能。由于下游健康结果的注释如此稀疏，可能需要重大工程来performance. With such sparse annotations of downstream health outcomes, significant engineering may be required to最佳地optimally使通用 SensorFM 嵌入适应每个端点。为此，人工智能语言模型推理问题和编写代码的能力提供了一种有前途的方法来加快模型开发，并部分弥合数据稀疏性的局限性。在这项工作中，我们建立了一个用于迭代解决方案发现的代理驱动框架，能够有效地将一般嵌入适应不同的健康领域，与线性探针相比，在区分性健康任务中显示出全面的好处。代理驱动的研究和科学发现是一个快速发展的领域（Aygün 等人，2025 年；Gottweis 等人，2026 年），未来的工作应继续探索这些方法可以在多大程度上被利用，为医疗保健中发现的稀疏标记数据提供好处。adapt the general SensorFM embeddings to each endpoint. Towards this, the capabilities of AI language models to reason over problems and write code presents a promising approach to expedite model development, and partially bridge the limitations of data sparsity. In this work we establish that an agent-driven framework for iterative solution discovery is able to efficiently adapt general embeddings to diverse health domains showing sweeping benefits across discriminative health tasks as compared to a linear probe. Agent driven research and scientific discovery is a rapidly evolving field (Aygün et al., 2025; Gottweis et al., 2026) and future work should continue to explore the extent to which these methods may be exploited to provide benefit to the sparsly labeled data found in healthcare.

3.4.对数字健康和临床监测的影响3.4. Implications for Digital Health and Clinical Monitoring

SensorFM 的功效对数字健康的未来具有重大影响。我们的结果支持从特定任务的可穿戴应用程序过渡到用于持续健康监测的通用界面。 SensorFM 无需复杂的、特定于任务的架构即可实现强大的预测准确性；将简单的线性探针应用于预训练的嵌入足以满足各种下游应用。通过为各种健康模型提供通用基础，预训练表示消除了针对每个单独结果的定制、复杂和端到端机器学习管道的需要，从而简化了数字健康中预测分析的部署。The efficacy of SensorFM carries significant implications for the future of digital health. Our results support a transition from task-specific wearable applications toward a general-purpose interface for continuous health monitoring. SensorFM achieves robust predictive accuracy without requiring complex, task-specific architectures; applying simple linear probes to the pretrained embeddings is sufficient for a wide variety of downstream applications. By providing a common substrate for various health models, pretrained representations eliminate the need for bespoke, complex, and end-to-end machine learning pipelines for every individual outcome, streamlining the deployment of predictive analytics in digital health.

此外，虽然传统医疗保健依赖于在临床就诊或实验室环境中捕获的间歇性“快照”测量，但可穿戴传感器可以对自由生活条件下的生理和行为进行密集的纵向观察。在这种情况下，可穿戴健康的通才模型可能有助于识别将从验证性测试或早期干预中受益的个人（Lubitz 等人，2022 年；Perez 等人，2019 年）。这对于直到晚期才保持无症状的病症尤其有价值（Liu 等人，2022 年；Yang 等人，2022 年）。然而，我们强调，这些预测旨在用于筛查、风险分层和纵向跟踪，而不是作为临床诊断的明确替代。Furthermore, while traditional healthcare relies on episodic, "snapshot" measurements captured during clinical visits or in laboratory settings, wearable sensors provide dense, longitudinal observations of physiology and behavior in free-living conditions. In this context, a generalist model for wearable health may be useful in identifying individuals who would benefit from confirmatory testing or early intervention (Lubitz et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2019). This is especially valuable for conditions that remain asymptomatic until advanced stages (Liu et al., 2022; Yang et al., 2022). We emphasize, however, that these predictions are intended for screening, risk stratification, and longitudinal tracking rather than as a definitive replacements for clinical diagnosis.

3.5.为个人健康提供更扎实的问答3.5. Towards More Grounded Question Answering for Personal Health

最近的研究强调了人们利用人工智能系统进行医疗问答的规模（Costa-Gomes 等人，2026），以及这些系统能够为查询提供反馈的功效（Breda 等人，2026；McDuff 等人，2025b）。可穿戴健康数据通过根据用户自己感知的生理和行为的测量结果提供答案，从而提供了提高用户查询响应质量的机会。我们对个人健康代理使用 SensorFM 作为工具的实验表明，将 SensorFM 预测纳入代理的上下文中会比缺乏这种额外上下文的基线系统带来统计上的显着改进。值得注意的是，尽管与临床事实相比，模型衍生的预测存在固有的缺陷，但我们观察到所产生的代理相互作用的质量没有统计学上的显着差异。展望未来，像我们这样的系统将人工智能教练与额外的基础相结合，可以提供更加个性化、主动且易于访问的指导，从而弥合持续健康感知和零星临床咨询之间的差距。Recent works have emphasized the scale on which people leverage AI systems for medical question answering (Costa-Gomes et al., 2026), and the efficacy with which these systems are able to provide feedback to queries (Breda et al., 2026; McDuff et al., 2025b). Wearable health data offers the opportunity to improve the quality of responses to user queries by grounding answers in measures of their own sensed physiology and behavior. Our experiments on the use of SensorFM as tool by a personal health agent demonstrate that incorporating SensorFM predictions into the agent’s context results in statistically significant improvements over baseline systems that lack this extra context. Notably, despite the inherent imperfections of model-derived predictions compared to the clinical ground truth, we observed no statistically significant difference in the quality of the resulting agentic interactions. Looking forward, systems like ours, which pair AI coaches with additional grounding, may enable the delivery of more personalized, proactive, and accessible guidance, bridging the gap between continuous health sensing and sporadic clinical consultations.

3.6.局限性和未来的工作3.6. Limitations and Future Work

我们承认这项研究有一些局限性。This study has several limitations that we acknowledge.第一First，消费者可穿戴设备在硬件和信号处理方面都是异构的，并且在如何处理方面的标准化有限。, consumer wearable devices are heterogeneous in both hardware and signal processing, and there is limited standardization in how

15

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

测量结果是跨平台得出的。尽管 SensorFM 是根据多个 Fitbit 和 Pixel Watch 设备的数据进行训练和评估的，但不能保证转移到其他设备生态系统，并且可能需要额外的调整。measurements are derived across platforms. Although SensorFM was trained and evaluated on data from multiple Fitbit and Pixel Watch devices, transfer to other device ecosystems is not guaranteed and would likely require additional adaptation.

第二Second，为了支持消费设备上的大规模建模，我们的输入表示使用一分钟聚合特征而不是原始传感器波形。这使得模型能够捕获长期的日常和昼夜节律背景，但它必然会放弃更精细的时间结构，例如逐次心跳的心脏变异性和亚秒级的运动模式，这些可能对某些临床任务提供信息。因此，需要做更多的工作来了解不同健康结果的最佳设置（采样频率、时间背景）。我们还注意到，由于存储占用和功率限制等考虑，更精细的信号也不一定会被存储。, to support large-scale modeling on consumer devices, our input representation uses one-minute aggregated features rather than raw sensor waveforms. This allows the model to capture long-range daily and circadian context, but it necessarily discards finer temporal structure, such as beat-to-beat cardiac variability and sub-second motion patterns, that may be informative for some clinical tasks. As such, more work is needed to understand the optimal settings (sampling frequency, temporal context) for different health outcomes. We additionally note that more granular signals are also not necessarily stored due to considerations such as storage footprints, and power constraints.

第三Third，收集与可穿戴数据配对的大型且可靠的下游标签仍然很困难。虽然这里研究的一些结果是实验室测试，但很大一部分依赖于自我报告的诊断、药物使用或筛查问卷。将连续筛选措施转换为二元结果可能会引入阈值相关噪声。因此，未来的工作应该根据临床验证的结果评估这些模型，包括电子健康记录（EHR）标签和金标准生理测量。, collecting large and reliable downstream labels paired with wearable data remains difficult. While some of the outcomes studied here are lab tests, a sizeable portion rely on self-reported diagnoses, medication use or screening questionnaires. Converting continuous screening measures into binary outcomes may introduce threshold-dependent noise. Future work should therefore evaluate these models against clinically verified outcomes, including electronic health record (EHR) labels and gold-standard physiological measurements.

第四Fourth，我们对 SensorFM 增强健康代理的评估仅限于静态、单轮交互范例。现实世界的临床咨询和人工智能代理交互本质上是多轮的，允许用户或医生提出后续问题以澄清歧义或收集缺失的背景信息。在我们的实验设计中，医生评估人员仅限于向他们提供的静态数据。此外，所使用的下游数据集本质上缺乏所有健康维度的全面真实标签（例如，缺少心理健康或睡眠目标）。虽然这种缺失凸显了我们方法的主要优势，即预测未知标签，但这也意味着评估人员的整体患者背景有限。尽管限制医生提出后续问题是一种有意的方法选择，旨在严格控制变量并防止评估偏差，但它确实限制了这些发现对动态、对话式现实世界部署的直接推广。, our evaluation of the SensorFM-augmented health agent was constrained to a static, single-turn interaction paradigm. Real-world clinical consultations and AI agent interactions are inherently multi-turn, allowing users or physicians to ask follow-up questions to clarify ambiguities or gather missing context. In our experimental design, physician evaluators were restricted solely to the static data presented to them. Furthermore, the downstream datasets utilized inherently lack comprehensive ground-truth labels across all health dimensions (e.g., missing mental health or sleep targets). While this missingness highlights a primary strength of our method, predicting unknown labels, it also means evaluators had limited holistic patient context. Although restricting physicians from asking follow-up questions was an intentional methodological choice to tightly control variables and prevent evaluation bias, it does limit the direct generalizability of these findings to dynamic, conversational real-world deployments.

最后Finally，预训练和下游评估人群都反映了 Fitbit 和 Pixel Watch 用户的特征，因此可能无法完全代表更广泛的美国或全球人群。尽管预训练队列涵盖了广泛的年龄、性别和体重指数，但可穿戴用户的数量并不一定代表美国的人口分布，例如女性用户的比例高于人口比例。因此，报告的绩效可能无法直接推广到总体人群，未来的评估将需要更有针对性的招募和针对特定亚组的验证。, both the pretraining and downstream evaluation populations reflect the characteristics of Fitbit and Pixel Watch users and therefore may not be fully representative of the broader United States or global population. Although the pretraining cohort spans a broad range of age, sex and BMI, the population of wearable users do not necessarily represent the United States population distribution, such as having a greater proportion of female users than the population ratio. Reported performance may therefore not generalize directly to the overall population and future evaluation will require more targeted recruitment and subgroup-specific validation.

4. 结论4. Conclusion

无处不在的可穿戴健康监测器为改善个人健康洞察提供了机会，从而推动预防性护理。为此，我们推出了 SensorFM，这是可穿戴健康的基础模型，可推广到不同的健康领域。虽然带有健康结果注释的数据很少见，但我们的方法直接从超过一万亿分钟的未标记多模式可穿戴传感器数据中学习，生成感知生理学的可靠表示，并对涵盖心血管、代谢和心理健康、睡眠、生活方式因素和生理相关人口统计等任务的预测益处。通过我们的分析，我们确定了这种规模化预训练的实用性，并进一步研究了 SensorFM 的标签效率、生成能力和潜在结构。为了使 SensorFM 嵌入更有效地适应不同的下游任务，我们提出了一个代理代码生成框架，该框架迭代地探索每个The ubiquity of wearable health monitors offers the opportunity to improve access to personal health insights and in so doing drive preventative care. To this end, we present SensorFM, a foundation model for wearable health which generalizes across diverse health domains. While data labeled with health outcome annotations are rare, our method learns directly from over one trillion minutes of unlabeled multimodal wearable sensor data, producing a robust representation of sensed physiology with predictive benefits on tasks spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, and mental health, sleep, lifestyle factors, and physiologically-relevant demographics. Through our analysis we establish the utility of this scaled pretraining, and further investigate the label efficiency, generative capabilities, and latent structure of SensorFM. To enable more efficient adaptation of the SensorFM embeddings to diverse downstream tasks, we present an agentic code generation framework that iteratively explores each

16

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

解空间。最后，为了了解 SensorFM 的端到端实用性，我们评估了向个人健康代理提供 SensorFM 预测的好处。总之，我们的工作代表了向更通用的可穿戴健康模型的转变，这使得跨健康领域的预测更加灵活，下游推理更加扎实，并有机会为用户提供有关自身健康的切实见解。solution space. Finally, to understand the end-to-end utility of SensorFM we assess the benefit of providing SensorFM predictions to a personal health agent. In summary, our work represents a shift towards more generalist models for wearable health, which enable more flexible prediction across health domains, more grounded downstream reasoning, and opportunities to provide users with tangible insights about their own health.

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

方法Methods

M.1。可穿戴基础模型的传感器信号M.1. Sensor Signals for Wearable Foundation Models

Fitbit Sense 2 和 Pixel Watch 2 使用与本研究主要相关的五个传感器：光电体积描记法 (PPG)、皮肤温度、加速度计、皮肤电活动 (EDA) 和高度计。从这些原始输入中，我们得出一组 34 个聚合信号（特征），详见表 ED.1。为了优化设备电池寿命和存储，不保留原始传感器数据；相反，我们依赖一分钟的聚合信号。在进行模型训练之前，我们测试了这些信号是否强共线性。附录 G 的图 ED.14 显示没有一对信号的相关性大于 0.8。The Fitbit Sense 2 and Pixel Watch 2 utilize five sensors of primary relevance to this study: Photoplethysmography (PPG), skin temperature, accelerometer, electrodermal activity (EDA), and an altimeter. From these raw inputs, we derive a set of 34 aggregate signals (features), detailed in Table ED.1. To optimize device battery life and storage, raw sensor data is not retained; instead, we rely on one-minute aggregate signals. We tested whether these signals were strongly co-linear before proceeding with model training. Figure ED.14 of Appendix G shows no pair of signals has a correlation greater than 0.8.

CRD 心血管。Cardiovascular.使用经过验证的算法从 1 Hz 的 PPG 信号中提取心率 (HR)（Nissen 等人，2022）。每分钟心率计算为非重叠一分钟窗口内瞬时心率的平均值。设备上的峰值检测算法可识别 R 波峰值以计算 RR 间隔。为了减轻噪音，使用稳健的统计方法计算标准 HRV 指标。我们推导出 RR 间期中位数、RR 间期的香农熵 (ShEnRR) 以及呼吸频率与心率的一致性。时域变异性指标——RR 间隔的标准差 (SDNN) 和连续差值的均方根 (RMSSD)——是使用 5[th] 和 95[th] 百分位数之间的 RR 间隔来计算的，以排除异常值。我们还计算 pNN20，即相差超过 20ms 的连续 RR 间隔的百分比。在频域中，我们提取极低 (VLF)、低 (LF) 和高 (HF) 频带中的功率、LF/HF 比以及功率谱的香农熵 (SpectralEn)。最后，“有效 RR”指标量化了包含有效 RR 间隔的 5 分钟窗口的百分比。Heart rate (HR) is extracted from the PPG signal at 1 Hz using a validated algorithm (Nissen et al., 2022). Per-minute HR is computed as the mean of the instantaneous heart rate across non-overlapping one-minute windows. An on-device peak detection algorithm identifies R-wave peaks to calculate RR intervals. To mitigate noise, standard HRV metrics are computed using robust statistical methods. We derive the median RR interval, the Shannon entropy of the RR intervals (ShEnRR), and the Coherence of breathing frequency to heart rate. Time-domain variability metrics—standard deviation of RR intervals (SDNN) and root mean squared of successive differences (RMSSD)—are calculated using RR intervals between the 5[th] and 95[th] percentiles to exclude outliers. We also compute pNN20, the percentage of successive RR intervals differing by more than 20ms.In the frequency domain, we extract the power in the Very Low (VLF), Low (LF), and High (HF) frequency bands, the LF/HF ratio, and the Shannon entropy of the power spectrum (SpectralEn). Finally, the "Valid RR" metric quantifies the percentage of the 5-minute window containing valid RR intervals.

产品生命周期管理PLM心肺。Cardiopulmonary.我们得出与血氧饱和度相关的两个特征。 SpO2 代表血氧饱和度水平，而 SpO2 置信度则提供读数的置信度。 SpO2 仅在静止期间计算；原始传感器数据经过过滤，以隔离加速度计方差较低的部分。 PPG 波形由卷积神经网络处理以提取特征，随后通过全连接层进行分类。我们还跟踪 SpO2 覆盖率，定义为具有有效 SpO2 数据的分钟百分比。We derive two features related to blood oxygen saturation. SpO2 represents the blood oxygen saturation level, while SpO2 Confidence provides the confidence level of the reading. SpO2 is computed exclusively during stationary periods; raw sensor data is filtered to isolate segments where accelerometer variance is low. The PPG waveform is processed by a convolutional neural network to extract features, which are subsequently classified via a fully connected layer. We also track SpO2 Coverage, defined as the percentage of the minute with valid SpO2 data.

SLP 睡觉吧。Sleep.睡眠指标是使用经过验证的多模式算法推断的，该算法将加速度计衍生的体动记录仪与 PPG 衍生的心率和 HRV 数据融合在一起。该模型将睡眠时期分为四个主要阶段：清醒、轻度、深度和快速眼动 (REM)。对于每个一分钟的窗口，我们记录每个阶段所花费的时间（以秒为单位）。Sleep metrics are inferred using a validated multi-modal algorithm that fuses accelerometer-derived actigraphy with PPG-derived heart rate and HRV data. The model classifies sleep epochs into four primary stages: Awake, Light, Deep, and Rapid Eye Movement (REM). For each one-minute window, we record the time spent in each of these stages in seconds.

MTN 运动。Motion.我们从 3 轴加速度计中提取十个特征来表征运动和身体活动。其中包括步数、轴平均值（3 轴数据的平均值）和峰度（3 轴均方根幅度）。复杂信号特征包括 Jerk 自相关（lag-1 自相关与能量的比率）、对数能量和对数能量比率。我们计算协方差作为 3 轴协方差条件数的估计。我们还检查第一个 3 轴主成分的零交叉，提取零交叉平均值（交叉之间的平均时间）和零交叉标准差。 （穿越之间时间的标准偏差）。此外，我们还导出了“睡眠系数”元数据特征，计算为 16 个对数刻度箱内 3 轴最大-最小范围的总和。从气压计中，我们计算海拔高度 St.Dev，它表示高度计读数的标准偏差（以 hP 为单位），以将垂直位移与大气漂移分开。We extract ten features from the 3-axis accelerometer to characterize motion and physical activity. These include the step count, Axis Mean (mean of the 3-axis data), and Kurtosis (of the 3-axis root mean squared magnitude).Complex signal features include Jerk Autocorrelation (ratio of lag-1 autocorrelation to energy), Log Energy, and Log Energy Ratio. We calculate the Covariance as an estimate of the condition number for the 3-axis covariance. We also examine the zero-crossings of the 1st 3-axis principal component, extracting both the Zero Crossing Average (mean time between crossings) and Zero Crossing St.Dev. (standard deviation of time between crossings).Additionally, we derive a "Sleep Coefficient" metadata feature, calculated as the sum of the 3-axis max-min range within 16 log-scaled bins.From the barometer, we compute the Altitude St.Dev., which represents the standard deviation of altimeter readings (in hP) to isolate vertical displacement from atmospheric drift.

SKN 皮肤表面。Skin Surface.该设备测量皮肤电导和温度以推断生理状态。皮肤电活动 (EDA) 传感器可测量皮肤电导水平 (SCL)，The device measures skin conductance and temperature to infer physiological states. The Electro-Dermal Activity (EDA) sensor measures Skin Conductance Level (SCL), which

与交感神经系统的兴奋相关。我们推导出“电导”特征，定义为线性主调 SCL 值拟合的中心（在correlates with sympathetic nervous system arousal. We derive the "Conductance" feature, defined as the center of the linear tonic SCL value fit (in𝜇西门子）。记录引线接触计数以跟踪传感器引线接触手腕的次数。同时，面向手腕的表面上的温度传感器对皮肤温度进行采样。我们将“温度”特征报告为每分钟皮肤温度的平均值（以°C为单位）。Siemens). Lead Contact Counts are recorded to track the number of times sensor leads contact the wrist.Concurrently, a temperature sensor on the wrist-facing surface samples skin temperature. We report the "Temperature" feature as the mean value of skin temperature (in °C) for the minute.

为了为我们的实验构建大型数据集，我们在 9 月 1 日期间对 500 万参与者的可穿戴数据进行了采样To build the large dataset for our experiments we sampled wearable data from 5 million participants during the period September 1[𝑠𝑡]2024年至9月1日2024 to September 1[𝑠𝑡]2025 年。由于参与者可以选择使用任何 Fitbit 或 Pixel Watch 设备，因此该数据集包含 2012 年至 2024 年期间发布的各种型号的数据（见表 ED.13）。最常见的设备是 Fitbit Inspire 3、Fitbit Charge 6、Fitbit Versa 2、3 和 4、Fitbit Sense 和 Pixel Watch 1、2、3。参与者自愿同意将其去识别化数据用于新的健康和保健产品和服务的研发，我们从中央 IRB 获得了二次研究豁免决定2025. As participants could opt-in using any Fitbit or Pixel Watch device, the dataset contains data from a wide range of models released between 2012 to 2024 (see Table ED.13). The most common devices were Fitbit Inspire 3, Fitbit Charge 6, Fitbit Versa 2, 3 and 4, Fitbit Sense and Pixel Watch 1, 2, 3. Participants provided voluntary consent for their de-identified data to be used for research and development of new health and wellness products and services and we obtained a secondary research exemption determination from a centralized IRB

为了创建一个最大化受试者数量的数据集，我们从 500 万受试者中随机抽取了 10 周的数据。从这组多达 3.5 亿人日的数据中，我们构建了一个包含超过 20 亿小时多模态传感器数据的预训练数据集。我们的预训练集总共包含超过To create a dataset that maximized the number of subjects we randomly sampled 10-weeks of data from each of the 5 million subjects. From this set of upto 350 million human-days, we construct a pretraining dataset encompassing over 2 billion hours of multimodal sensor data. In total our pretrain set contains over一万亿分钟one trillion minutes从一组五个可穿戴传感器观察到的分钟分辨率数据。在准备建模数据时，在此预训练数据集上计算每个传感器信号的全局归一化参数（平均值和标准差）。这些参数用于标准化（z 分数）预训练和下游数据（图 ED.2）。除了这个预训练集之外，还使用来自 10,000 名独立受试者的一周数据进行验证，并使用来自另一组独立的 10,000 名受试者的一周数据进行测试。of minute-resolution data observed from a suite of five wearable sensors. In preparing our data for modeling, global normalization parameters (mean and standard deviation) for each of the sensor signals were computed on this pretraining dataset. These parameters were used to normalize (z-score) the pretraining and downstream data (Figure ED.2). In addition to this pretraining set, one week of data from an independent 10,000 subjects were used for validation, and one week of data from another independent set of 10,000 subjects were used for test.

(2026). The study was approved by Advarra (Pro00074093). We enrolled 4,416 participants, of which 1,086 had complete data (at least 14 days of data wearable data, lab results, and completed demographics) and were included in our analysis. The study, which spanned a maximum of 70 days, was designed to gauge the feasibility of leveraging wrist-worn wearable devices to develop algorithms for assessing metabolic health, cardiovascular health, and respiratory health deriving to biological age, and regressing to blood biomarkers. Specifically, participants completed surveys, obtained a one-time blood test, and were asked to continuously wear their wearable device for the duration of the study. Table ED.3 shows the numbers of people with confirmed responses for each of the data types (including lab reports and self-reported health history.

研究人口。Study Population.该研究仅限于居住在美国、年龄在 21 至 80 岁之间、拥有心率感应设备的 Fitbit 用户。这些用户被要求拥有至少三个月前的数据，并且这些天中至少有 75% 的时间使用过数据。The study was limited to Fitbit users with a heart-rate sensing capable device, living in the US, and aged 21 - 80. These users were required to have at least three months prior data, with use on at least 75% of these days.

自我报告人口统计、生物识别和病史调查问卷。Self-Report Demographics, Biometrics, and Medical History Questionnaires.参与者完成了调查，报告了他们的人口统计数据（年龄、性别、种族、体重、身高）、血压、腰围、药物、诊断状况、健康习惯和管理策略。所有收集的数据都列在“方法”部分中。Participants completed surveys reporting their demographic data (age, sex, ethnicity, weight, height), blood pressure, waist circumference, medications, diagnosed conditions, health habits and management strategies. All collected data are listed in the Methods section.

血液检测板。Blood Panels.参与者在禁食至少 8 小时后于清晨从 Quest Diagnostics 进行血液检测，以尽量减少太阳昼夜周期的影响。测试的血液生物标志物包括胰岛素、HbA1c、综合代谢组（包括葡萄糖）、脂质（总胆固醇、甘油三酯、HDL、LDL）、全血计数、hs-CRP、GGT 和总睾酮。测试结果还用于计算弗雷明汉风险评分，以预测 10 年心血管疾病Participants obtained blood testing early in the morning from Quest Diagnostics after fasting for at least 8 hours in order to minimize the effect of solar diurnal cycle. Tested blood biomarkers included insulin, HbA1c, Comprehensive Metabolic Panel (including glucose), lipids (total cholesterol, triglycerides, HDL, LDL), Complete Blood Count, hs-CRP, GGT, and total testosterone. Test results were also used to calculate a Framingham Risk Score for 10-year prediction of cardiovascular

M.2.2.2。睡眠M.2.2.2. Sleep

我们设计了一项前瞻性观察研究，并于 2023 年 8 月至 2024 年 1 月期间招募了来自美国所有 50 个州的 10,000 多名参与者。该研究得到了中央 IRB (Advarra) 的批准 (Pro00069849)。该研究旨在支持改进 Fitbit 睡眠评分，并更好地了解睡眠、第二天睡眠和健康结果（例如警觉性、情绪等）之间的关系。另一个目标是利用这些数据来开发睡眠和健康模型（例如昼夜节律），使用户能够更好地了解他们的睡眠并提供更个性化的建议。每位参与者的研究持续时间为 15 天（14 晚），在此期间，参与者被要求持续佩戴 Fitbit（充电期间除外）。此外，研究数据中还包括长达一个月的 Fitbit 之前数据。We designed a prospective observational study and recruited over 10,000 participants from all 50 states of the United States between August 2023 and January 2024. The study was approved by a centralized IRB (Advarra) (Pro00069849). The study was designed to support improvements to the Fitbit Sleep Score and to better understand the relationship between sleep, next day sleep, and health outcomes (e.g., alertness, mood, etc.). An additional aim was to leverage the data to develop models of sleep and health (e.g. circadian rhythm), enabling users to better understand their sleep and allowing for more personalized recommendations. The study duration was 15 days (14 nights) per participant during which time participants were asked to continuously wear their Fitbit except during charging. Additionally, up to one month of prior Fitbit data was included in the study data.

研究人口。Study Population.这项研究仅限于当前的 Fitbit 用户，他们的设备包含心率传感器，并且拥有能够安装 Google Health Studies 应用程序的 Android 智能手机。参与者还被要求年龄在 18 至 88 岁之间，并且位于美国。The study was limited to current Fitbit users whose devices included a heart rate sensor and who owned an Android smartphone capable of installing the Google Health Studies application. Participants were further required to be age 18 - 88, and located in the United States.

基线和研究后调查问卷。Baseline and Post-Study Questionnaires.参与者在基线时完成了一系列自我报告问卷，包括人口统计、睡眠习惯、健康习惯、睡眠环境、国家睡眠基金会的睡眠满意度工具（Ohayon 等人，2019 年）和早晚调查（Horne 和 Ostberg，1976 年）。Participants completed a battery of self-report questionnaires at baseline including demographics, Sleep Habits, Health Habits, Sleep Environment, the National Sleep Foundation’s Sleep Satisfaction Tool (Ohayon et al., 2019), and the MorningnessEveningness Survey (Horne and Ostberg, 1976).

早晚调查。Morning and Evening Survey.每天早上，参与者被要求完成一项包含 5 项的调查，报告他们前一天晚上的睡眠情况。每天晚上，参与者被要求报告当天的活动，包括食物摄入量、锻炼和其他活动。Each morning, participants were asked to complete a 5-item survey reporting on their sleep the previous night. Each evening, participants were asked to report on that day’s activities including food intake, exercise, and other activities.

警觉性和情绪调查。Alertness and Mood Surveys.每天最多三次，参与者被要求完成关于他们的警觉性和情绪的生态瞬时评估（EMA）问题。调查安排在上午、下午和晚上，并根据每个参与者的大致睡眠时间表进行个性化。Up to three times daily, participants were asked to complete Ecological Momentary Assessment (EMA) questions on their alertness and mood. Surveys were timed for mid-morning, afternoon, and evening, and were personalized for each participants’ approximate sleep schedule.

警觉任务。Alertness Tasks.每天最多三次，参与者被要求完成两项旨在衡量警觉性的标准化任务。其中包括三分钟的反应时间任务，参与者将点击智能手机屏幕以响应某些刺激，以及五分钟的凝视任务，当刺激出现在屏幕上时，跟踪参与者的凝视。Up to three times daily, participants were asked to complete two standardized tasks meant to gauge alertness. These included a three-minute reaction time task where participants would tap their smartphone screen in response to some stimuli, and a five-minute gaze task where participants’ gaze was tracked as stimuli were presented on screen.

补偿。Compensation.如果受试者在实验方案的 15 天/夜晚中至少尝试了 12 天/夜晚（80%），他们将获得 25 美元的 Google 商品购买代码作为补偿。Subjects were compensated with a $25 Google Merchandise Purchase Code if they attempted at least 12 of 15 ( 80%) of the days/nights of the protocol.

M.2.2.3。心理健康M.2.2.3. Mental Health

我们设计了一项前瞻性观察性研究，并在美国招募了 7,500 名 Fitbit 用户（McDuff 等人，2024 年；Winbush 等人，2025 年）。该研究得到了俄勒冈大学 IRB 的批准（MOD00000379）。该研究旨在调查数字设备使用、基于传感器的测量（包括行为和生理信号）以及自我报告的心理健康和福祉测量之间的模式和关系。每个参与者的研究持续时间为四个星期，并且在整个四个星期期间包括可穿戴设备。该研究和We designed a prospective, observational study and recruited 7,500 Fitbit users in the United States (McDuff et al., 2024; Winbush et al., 2025). The study was approved by the IRB of the University of Oregon (MOD00000379). The study was designed to investigate patterns and relationships between digital device use, sensor based measures (including both behavioral and physiological signals), and self-reported measures of mental health and well-being. The study duration was fourweeks long per participant and included a wearable for the complete four-week period. The study and

27

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

招募的目的是增加代表性不足群体的参与，这些群体按种族和民族（例如白种人、非裔美国人、亚洲人、拉丁裔/拉丁裔、美洲原住民/土著居民）、出生时的生物性别（女性；男性）、年龄（18 - 40 岁；40 岁以上）、性取向/性别认同（异性恋；LGBTQIA+）来定义。recruitment were designed to increase participation of under-represented groups, as defined by race and ethnicity (e.g., Caucasian, African American, Asian, Latina/Latino, Native Americans/ Indigenous Populations), biological sex at birth (Female; Male), age (18 - 40; over 40), sexual orientation/gender identity (Heterosexual; LGBTQIA+).

研究人口。Study Population.该研究仅限于 18-80 岁且拥有能够安装 Google Health Studies 应用程序的 Android 智能手机的参与者。参与者还被要求没有严重限制活动和身体活动的重大健康状况。该群体中的一部分拥有 Fitbit 设备，他们被邀请分享他们的 Fitbit 数据。The study was limited to participants aged 18-80 with an Android smartphone capable of installing the Google Health Studies application. Participants were further required to be free of major health conditions which severely restricted mobility and physical activity. A subset of this group, who owned a Fitbit device, were invited to share their Fitbit data.

基线和研究后调查问卷。Baseline and Post-Study Questionnaires.参与者在基线和研究结论中完成了一系列自我报告调查问卷，如下：（仅基线）人口统计调查问卷、睡眠和健康习惯调查问卷、患者健康问卷（PHQ-8）（Kroenke等人，2009年）、广泛性焦虑症量表（GAD-7）（Löwe等人，2008年；Spitzer等人，2006年）、患者报告结果测量信息系统 (PROMIS) 睡眠障碍和睡眠相关障碍简表（Cella 等人，2010 年；Yu 等人，2012 年）、缩短智能手机成瘾量表（SAS）（Kwon 等人，2013 年）、感知压力量表（PSS）（Cohen 等人，1983 年）。Participants completed a battery of self-report questionnaires at baseline and study conclusion, as follows: (Baseline only) Demographics questionnaire, Sleep and Health Habits questionnaire, Patient Health Questionnaire (PHQ-8) (Kroenke et al., 2009), Generalized Anxiety Disorder Scale (GAD-7) (Löwe et al., 2008; Spitzer et al., 2006), Patient Reported Outcomes Measurement Information System (PROMIS) Sleep Disturbance & Sleep Related Impairment short form (Cella et al., 2010; Yu et al., 2012), Shortened Smartphone Addiction Scale (SAS) (Kwon et al., 2013), Perceived Stress Scale (PSS) (Cohen et al., 1983).

生态瞬时评估调查。Ecological Momentary Assessment Surveys.在研究的第一周和最后一周，参与者被要求填写全天的三份 EMA 调查。每个 EMA 都会评估参与者的五种情绪情绪（During the first and last week of the study, participants were asked to fill out three EMA surveys spread throughout the day. Each EMA assessed the participant’s mood across five affects (快乐happy , 冷静calm , 着急的anxious , 悲伤sad, 和, and强调stressed），每个项目均采用单选 5 项李克特量表进行评估，涵盖), each assessed on a single-select 5-item Likert scale, spanning一点也不not at all to 非常very。参与者还被要求报告他们与谁相处的时间最长（自上一届 EMA 以来）。包括选项. Participants were also asked to report who they had spent the most time with (since the previous EMA). Options included独自一人Alone , 朋友Friends , 家庭Family , 配偶/伴侣Spouse/Partner , 同事Co-workers , 同学Co-students .

每日状态报告。Daily Status Reports.在整个四个星期的时间里，每天早上，参与者都会被提示报告他们过去一天的感受。参与者通过单选 5 项李克特量表进行回答Each morning, throughout the four-week period, participants were prompted to report how they had been feeling over the past day. Participants responded via a single-select 5-item Likert scale spanning非常糟糕very bad to 非常好very good .

移动和可穿戴数据。Mobile and Wearable Data.在为期四个星期的研究过程中，从参与者的设备纵向收集数据。手机指标包括屏幕开启时间、按应用程序类别划分的使用时间、电池状态和手机解锁次数。还收集了地理位置的汇总测量值，将参与者的位置分为以下任一位置：Data was longitudinally collected from participants’ devices over the course of the four-week study. Mobile phone metrics included screen on time, usage time by application category, battery status, and number of phone unlocks. Aggregated measures of geolocation were also collected, binning the participants locations at either being at家home , 工作work, 或, or其他other。对于一部分参与者，可穿戴传感器数据也被连续记录。请注意，本文中描述的传感器数据是指来自这些可穿戴设备的数据，而不是来自手机的数据。. For a subset of participants, wearable sensor data was also logged continuously. Note that sensor data, described in this work, refers to the data from these wearable devices rather than from mobile phones.

补偿。Compensation.受试者参加抽奖活动，赢取 50 美元的礼品卡，中奖概率为 11.5%。资格条件为：1) 在研究开始时同意并启用传感器收集，2) 完成研究前评估，3) 完成至少 7 个研究日（累计一周）的日常状态评估，4) 完成研究后评估。Subjects were entered into a raffle to win a $50 gift card with an 11.5% win probability. The conditions for eligibility were to: 1) consent and enable sensor collection at study start, 2) complete the pre-study assessments, 3) complete daily status assessments for a minimum of seven study days (one week, cumulatively), and 4) to complete the post-study assessments.

M.2.2.4。下游数据统计M.2.2.4. Downstream Data Statistics

While our datasets represent a broad group from the global (pretraining) and US (downstream) populace there are some areas in which representation is still lacking, pretraining data is skewed towards women who are more frequent adopters of Fitbit devices and White/Caucasians.
