32

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

纳拉扬斯瓦米等人，2025；徐等人，2025）。对这些长上下文传感器流进行建模时的一个重要考虑因素是数据中固有的碎片，由于电荷丢失事件、间歇性移除设备、传感器或环境噪声或各种设备操作模式等原因而发生丢失（见图 ED.1）。为此，我们设计了一种预训练技术（参见图 ED.2），该技术可以修补多模态数据输入，并用掩码标记替换缺少观测值的补丁。遵循 Xu 等人提出的自适应和继承掩蔽（AIM）策略。 （2025），我们的方法将完整应用的掩模视为缺失数据掩模和为重建预训练目标生成的人工掩模的并集。利用令牌丢失和注意力屏蔽的两阶段令牌屏蔽用于确保现实世界缺失的固有可变性下的灵活性，同时保持计算效率。有关 AIM 的更多详细信息可以在原始论文中找到（Xu 等人，2025）。Narayanswamy et al., 2025; Xu et al., 2025). An important consideration in modeling these longcontext sensor streams is the fragmentation inherent in the data, with missingness occuring for reasons such as loss of charge events, intermittent removal of the device, sensor or environmental noise, or various device operation modes (see Figure ED.1). To this end, we designed a pretraining technique (see Figure ED.2) that patches multimodal data inputs and replaces patches with missing observations with mask tokens. Following the Adaptive and Inherited Masking (AIM) strategy introduced by Xu et al. (2025), our method treats the full applied mask as the union of the missing data mask and the artificial mask generated for the reconstruction pretraining objective. Two stage token masking, leveraging token dropout and attention masking, is used to ensure flexibility under the inherent variability of real world missingness while retaining computational efficiency. Additional details regarding AIM maybe found in the original paper (Xu et al., 2025).

具体来说，我们的模型利用 ViT-1D 编码器主干（Dosovitskiy 等人，2021），其超参数如表 ED.4 中所述。在基于 AIM 的预训练期间，潜在嵌入通过 ViT-1D 解码器传递，该解码器学习重建消融的输入。屏蔽标记通过可学习的屏蔽标记来表示。为了表示输入的时间和传感器特征维度，对标记应用了 2D 加性位置编码。学习了与特征维度相对应的编码维度的一半。与时间维度相对应的位置编码维度的一半是一维正弦编码（Vaswani 等人，2017），除了与循环日期时间特征相对应的八个嵌入特征（Spathis 等人，2021）：一小时中的分钟 [0, 59]、一天中的小时 [0, 23]、一周中的一天 [0, 6] 和一年中的一天 [0, 364]。所有 SensorFM 模型均使用均方误差 (MSE) 重建损失进行预训练。损失仅根据最初观察到的（即没有丢失）的屏蔽令牌来计算。Specifically, our model leverages a ViT-1D encoder backbone (Dosovitskiy et al., 2021), with the hyperparameters described in Table ED.4. During AIM-based pretraining the latent embeddings are passed through a ViT-1D decoder which learns to reconstruct the ablated input. Masked tokens are represented through a learnable mask token. To represent temporal and sensor feature dimensions of the input, 2D additive positional encodings are applied to the tokens. Half of the encoding dimensions, corresponding to the feature dimension, are learned. Half of the positional encoding dimensions, corresponding to the temporal dimension, are 1D sinusoidal encodings (Vaswani et al., 2017), except for eight embedding features which correspond to cyclic datetime features (Spathis et al., 2021): minute of the hour [0, 59], hour of the day [0, 23], day of the week [0, 6], and day of the year [0, 364]. All SensorFM models were pretrained with a mean-squared error (MSE) reconstruction loss. The loss was calculated exclusively on the masked tokens which were originally observed (i.e., not missing).

M.3.2。数据整理和预处理M.3.2. Data Curation and Preprocessing

虽然大型预训练数据集对于构建基础模型至关重要，但有证据表明严格的管理流程可以显着提高模型质量（Grattafiori 等人，2024）。为了确保我们万亿分钟数据语料库的完整性，我们精心实施了一条管道While large pretraining datasets are essential for building foundation models, evidence suggests that rigorous curation pipelines significantly improve model quality (Grattafiori et al., 2024). To ensure the integrity of our trillion-minute data corpus, we carefully implemented a pipeline for

33

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

预训练和下游任务。首先，为了协调不同的传感器流，所有输入都被重新采样为统一的一分钟分辨率，并纠正时区偏移。然后我们应用生理掩蔽来消除非生物伪影。皮肤电导水平 (SCL) 值限制在 0–60 的生理范围内both pretraining and downstream tasks. First, to harmonize diverse sensor streams, all inputs were resampled to a uniform one-minute resolution and corrected for timezone offsets. We then applied physiological masking to eliminate non-biological artifacts. Skin conductance level (SCL) values were restricted to the physiological range of 0–60𝜇西门子和皮肤温度读数被限制在 0 − 41[°] C。 血氧饱和度 (Siemens, and skin temperature readings were constrained to 0 − 41[◦] C. Blood oxygen saturation (𝑆𝑝𝑂2) 低于 70% 或标记为无效的值被视为缺失，以消除虚假丢弃，而超过 100% 的值则受到限制。对于心率变异性，方差指标（RMSSD、SDNN）的上限为 125 毫秒，以限制极端异常值的影响，并且如果从具有以下条件的窗口导出，则所有 HRV 指标均无效：2) values below 70% or flagged as invalid were treated as missing to remove spurious drops, while values exceeding 100% were capped. For heart rate variability, variance metrics (RMSSD, SDNN) were capped at 125 ms to limit the impact of extreme outliers, and all HRV metrics were nullified if derived from windows with<20% 有效节拍间隔。此外，在设备被检测为脱离手腕期间收集的数据被丢弃，以防止噪声注入。最后，为了稳定训练动态以对抗剩余的异常值，所有特征都使用从预训练语料库导出的全局每个特征统计数据进行 z 分数标准化，并剪裁到区间 [−520% valid inter-beat intervals. Additionally, data collected during periods where the device was detected as off-wrist were discarded to prevent noise injection. Finally, to stabilize training dynamics against remaining outliers, all features were z-score normalized using global per-feature statistics derived from the pretraining corpus and clipped to the interval [−5,5]。我们应用了滑动窗口，连续窗口按可变间隔移动。这种转变是在 8 到 24 分钟之间随机进行的，以增强生成的数据窗口的多样性。固有数据缺失超过 80% 的窗口被删除。总共，我们最大的预训练数据量包含 1755]. We applied a sliding window with consecutive windows shifted by a variable interval. This shift was randomized between 8 and 24 minutes to enhance the diversity of the generated data windows. The windows with more than 80% of inherent data missingness were removed. In total, our largest pretrain data volume contains 175, 062 ,146 个全天样本。146 day-long samples.

M.3.3。预训练M.3.3. Pretraining

我们使用自监督屏蔽自动编码器框架来预训练我们的模型，该框架旨在从不完整的输入中重建数据。我们的掩蔽策略 AIM（Xu et al., 2025）可以处理固有（“继承”）缺失和合成（“人工”）掩蔽。至关重要的是，重建损失仅在最初存在地面实况值的人工屏蔽补丁上计算，忽略继承的缺失。人工掩蔽策略采用混合概率方法来模拟多模态传感器数据碎片的多种现实世界模式。具体来说，对于给定的样本，我们的模型随机应用以下掩码之一：80% 随机补丁掩码、50% 时间块掩码（模拟设备移除）和 50% 模态块掩码（模拟传感器丢失）。该模型使用的补丁大小为 [20We pretrain our model using a self-supervised masked autoencoder framework designed to reconstruct data from incomplete inputs. Our masking strategy AIM (Xu et al., 2025) handles both inherent ("inherited") missingness and synthetic ("artificial") masking. Crucially, the reconstruction loss is computed exclusively on the artificially masked patches where ground truth values were originally present, ignoring inherited missingness. The artificial masking strategy employs a mixed probabilistic approach to simulate diverse real-world modes of multimodal sensor data fragmentation. Specifically, for a given sample, our model randomly applies one of the following masks: 80% random patch masking, 50% temporal block masking (simulating device removal), and 50% modality block masking (simulating sensor dropout). The model utilizes a patch size of [20,1]，每个传感器功能处理 20 分钟窗口。训练的全局批量大小为 4096，AdamW 优化器使用权重衰减为 1 × 10[−][4] 。我们利用余弦退火学习率调度器，其基本学习率为 5 × 10[−][4]，线性预热等于步数的 5%。我们训练最多1], processing 20-minute windows per sensor feature. Training was conducted with a global batch size of 4096 and an AdamW optimizer using with a weight decay of 1 × 10[−][4] . We leverage a cosine annealing learning rate scheduler with a base learning rate of 5 × 10[−][4] and a linear warm up equal to 5% of the number of steps. We train for a maximum of𝑁 = 1 , 000 ,000 步。我们训练所有模型 240,000 步。唯一的例外是当模型表现出过度拟合时。在这些情况下，预训练提前终止（具体来说，具有 50k 受试者的基本模型为 100k 步骤，具有 5k 受试者的基本模型为 80k 步骤，以及具有 5k 受试者的小模型为 60k 步骤）。000 steps. We train all models for 240,000 steps. The only exceptions occurr when models exhibit overfitting. In these instances, pre-training was terminated early (specifically, at 100k steps for base model with 50k subjects, 80k steps for base Model with 5k subjects, and 60k steps for small model with 5k subjects).

M.3.4。区分性的培训后和评估M.3.4. Discriminative Post-Training and Evaluation

我们在冻结的预训练 SensorFM 编码器顶部独立微调了每个下游任务的轻量级头，以防止学习目标混淆。我们的评估套件总共包含 35 个判别任务（二元任务和回归任务），表 ED.3 中有更详细的描述。所有任务都对应于个人级别的预测，其中单个标签适用于整个参与者（例如，高血压诊断、年龄）。因此，我们汇总了每个人在所有非屏蔽（无继承缺失）标记中的嵌入，计算每个人在所有数据中的嵌入的平均值和标准偏差。为了更好地将嵌入的方差与下游标签的稀疏性相匹配，我们将 SensorFM 聚合嵌入减少到 50 个主成分 (PCA-50)。对于包含人口统计特征的任务，年龄、性别、BMI 和种族在线性探测之前与减少的嵌入连接起来。We independently fine-tuned a light-weight head on top of the frozen pretrained SensorFM encoder for each downstream task to prevent conflating learning objectives. Our evaluation suite comprises a total of 35 discriminative tasks (binary and regression) which are described in more detail in Table ED.3. All the tasks correspond to person-level predictions where a single label applies to the entire participant (e.g., hypertension diagnosis, age). As such we aggregated the embeddings per person across all non-masked (no inherited missingness) tokens, computing the mean and standard deviations of each person’s embeddings across all days of their data. To better match the variance of the embeddings with the sparsity of downstream labels, we reduce the SensorFM aggregated embeddings to 50 principal components (PCA-50). For tasks where demographic features are included, age, sex, BMI, and race are concatenated with the reduced embeddings prior to the linear probe.

对于分类任务，我们将线性探针附加到冷冻的减少的嵌入上For classification tasks, we attached a linear probe to the reduced embeddings from the frozen

34

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

编码器，以及可选的人口统计特征。我们使用带有 AdamW 优化器的逻辑回归头（学习率 5 × 10[−][3] ，权重衰减 1 × 10[−][4] ）进行 500 个步骤的训练。我们使用曲线下的接收器操作特征区域来评估分类性能（encoder, and optionally demographic features. We trained using a logistic regression head with an AdamW optimizer (learning rate 5 × 10[−][3] , weight decay 1 × 10[−][4] ) for 500 steps. We evaluate classification performance with the Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (𝑅𝑂𝐶𝐴𝑈𝐶）和) and the𝐹1 分。对于回归任务，我们采用了带有线性回归头的类似设置，通过皮尔逊相关性评估性能（1 score. For regression tasks, we employed a similar setup with a linear regression head, evaluating performance via Pearson correlation (𝑟）和平均绝对误差（) and Mean Absolute Error (𝑀𝐴𝐸）。为了确保稳健的评估，所有呈现的结果都是采用五倍交叉验证设置的聚合外折叠 (OOF) 性能。请注意，由于每个受试者都占一个数据样本，因此这些折叠自然是独立于个人的。每个分割的主题在所有下游保持固定。此外，虽然 F1 和 MAE 采用算术平均值和标准差进行聚合，但 Pearson (). To ensure robust evaluation, all presented results are the aggregated out-of-fold (OOF) performance with a five-fold cross validation setup. Note as each subject accounts for a singe data sample these folds are naturally person independent. The subjects per split remain fixed across all downstreams. Additionally, while F1 and MAE are aggregated with arithmetic mean and standard deviation, Pearson (𝑟）和) and𝑅𝑂𝐶𝐴𝑈𝐶在变换空间中聚合以解释其偏度； z 变换空间中的 Pearson 相关性和 Logit 变换空间中的 ROC AUC（在反向变换之前）。还在变换空间中计算标准差并进行反向变换以给出不对称误差值。were aggregated in transform spaces to account for their skewness; Pearson correlation in the z-transform space, and ROC AUC the logit-transform space, before being back-transformed. Standard deviation was also calculated in the transformed space and back-transformed to give asymmetric error values.

M.3.5。生成评估M.3.5. Generative Evaluation

由于 SensorFM 是使用类似 MAE（He 等人，2022；Xu 等人，2025）目标进行训练的，因此它自然地表现出开箱即用的生成能力，使其能够填充和扩展未观察到的多模态传感器数据。为了评估这些生成能力，我们制定了以下生成任务。Since SensorFM was trained with an MAE-like (He et al., 2022; Xu et al., 2025) objective, it naturally exhibits out-of-box generative capabilities which allow it to infill and extend unobserved multimodal sensor data. To evaluate these generative capabilities we formulate the following generative tasks.随机插补Random Imputation掩盖信号和时间上 80% 的总标记，模拟通用随机噪声（例如，随机时间丢失的随机信号）。masks out 80% of total tokens across signals and time, emulating generic random noise (e.g., random signals missing at random times).时间插补Temporal Imputation和and时间外推法Temporal Extrapolation在序列的中间或末尾消融长度为 [10、30、60 分钟] 的连续时间窗口，模拟间歇性移除、电荷丢失事件等（例如，所有传感器特征在连续时间块内丢失）。ablation of a contiguous temporal window of length [10, 30, 60 minutes], either in the middle or at the end of the sequence, emulating intermittent removal, loss of charge events, etc. (e.g., all sensor features missing for a contiguous block of time).信号插补Signal Imputation屏蔽一组随机 [2/26, 6/26, 12/26] 信号通道的所有时间点，模拟丢失的传感器通道（例如，各种传感器负载、传感器丢失、非随机丢失）。重建性能是通过计算最初观察到的屏蔽标记的均方误差 (MSE) 来计算的，仅对具有基本事实的数据点进行平均。 95% 的置信区间是通过引导 100 次迭代的生成误差来生成的。表 ED.10 列出了该评估的结果。masks all time points for a random set of [2/26, 6/26, 12/26] signal channels, emulating missing sensor channels (e.g., various sensor loadouts, sensor dropout, non-random missingness). Reconstruction performance was calculated with mean squared error (MSE) calculated originally observed masked tokens, averaging only over the data points that have a ground truth. 95% confidence intervals were generated by bootstrapping the generative errors across 100 iterations. The results of this evaluation are presented in Table ED.10.

为了更好地了解这些生成功能的实用性，我们评估了 SensorFM 重建部分观察到的数据以提供更有意义的每日摘要统计数据的能力。具体来说，我们通过屏蔽所有传感器特征的连续 1 小时持续时间来模拟缺失。然后我们使用 SensorFM 来重建这些缺失的数据。我们预测每日指标预测To better contextualize the utility of these generative capabilities, we evaluate SensorFM’s ability to reconstruct partially observed data to provide more meaningful daily summary statistics. Specifically, we simulate missingness by masking a contiguous 1-hour duration across all sensor features. We then use SensorFM to reconstruct this missing data. We predict daily metric predictions across步骤Steps , 睡眠阶段分钟数Sleep Stage Minutes , 锻炼时间Exercise Minutes , SPO2 水平分钟数SPO2 Level Minutes, 和, and手腕温度水平 分钟Wrist Temperature Level Minutes。我们将 SensorFM 与真实数据、未消融数据以及基线与不填补缺失的方法进行比较。将数据丢失一小时后恢复的总分钟数与真实情况进行比较，并通过 100 次引导迭代生成 95% 的置信区间。该实验的结果列于表ED.11中。. We compare SensorFM to the ground truth, un-ablated data, and baseline against a method which does not infill missingness. Total recovered minutes from the hour of data loss were compared to the ground truth, with a 95% confidence intervals generated from 100 bootstrap iterations. The results of this experiment are presented in Table ED.11.

如方法 M.2.1 中所述，生成评估和每日指标估计均在源自 10 个独立测试集的情况下报告As mentioned in Methods M.2.1, both the generative evaluation and the daily metrics estimation are reported on an independent test set derived from 10,000 个科目。000 subjects.

M.3.6。工程基线特征M.3.6. Engineered Baseline Features

为了将 SensorFM 嵌入的性能置于上下文中，我们与基于相同传感器流派生的工程特征进行训练的监督模型进行了比较。这些工程特性在附录中的表 ED.14 中进行了详细描述，并在下面进行了高级描述。To contextualize the performance of SensorFM embeddings, we compare against supervised models trained on engineered features derived from the same sensor streams. These engineered features are described in detail in Table ED.14 in the Appendix, and are described in a high level below.

对于每个参与者日，我们遵循可穿戴研究和时间生物学中的既定方法，通过使用每日汇总统计数据聚合 24 小时窗口内每 34 分钟的传感器特征（表 ED.1）来计算固定长度的汇总向量。我们提取了 20 个不同的For each participant-day, we compute a fixed-length summary vector by aggregating each of the 34 minutely sensor features (Table ED.1) over the 24-hour window using daily summary statistics, following established methodologies in wearable research and chronobiology. We extracted 20 distinct

35

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

每个通道的特征（总共 680 个）捕捉分布、波动和时间生物学动态。为了保留有关数据碎片的信息，在进行任何插补之前计算每个通道的原始数据的丢失率。然后通过线性插值来解决丢失的数据，并在序列的开头和结尾处进行后向/前向填充，以确保连续的时间导数。features per channel (680 total) capturing distributional, volatile, and chronobiological dynamics. To preserve information about data fragmentation, the missing rate was calculated on the raw data for each channel prior to any imputation. Missing data were then resolved via linear interpolation with back/forward filling at the start and ends of sequences to ensure continuous temporal derivatives.

  • 分布和行为：缺失和信号稀疏被量化为硬件故障/行为表型（Choi 等人，2011；Torous 等人，2017）。通过平均值、中值、IQR、偏度、峰度、CV 和 RMS 捕获信号离散度。为了稳健地捕捉高频伪影的生理极端情况，使用第 5 个和第 95 个百分位数代替绝对最小值和最大值。Distributional and behavioral: Missingness and signal sparsity were quantified as hardware failure / behavioral phenotypes (Choi et al., 2011; Torous et al., 2017). Signal dispersion was captured via the mean, median, IQR, skewness, kurtosis, CV, and RMS. To robustly capture physiological extremes against high-frequency artifacts, 5th and 95th percentiles were used in lieu of absolute minimums and maximums.

  • 波动性：使用平均绝对分钟差异和连续差异均方根 (RMSSD) 来测量短期变化率（Shaffer 和 Ginsberg，2017），这是数字表型分析中历元变异性的既定指标。Volatility: Short-term rate-of-change was measured using mean absolute minute-to-minute differences and the root mean square of successive differences (RMSSD) (Shaffer and Ginsberg, 2017), an established metric for epoch-to-epoch variability in digital phenotyping.

  • 形态：信号碎片和带宽通过以均值为中心的过零率和 Hjorth 复杂度进行量化（Hjorth，1970）。选择这些时域度量是为了有效捕获信号的平均频率和带宽，而不需要对纵向数据进行计算密集型傅里叶变换。Morphology: Signal fragmentation and bandwidth were quantified via the mean-centered Zero Crossing Rate and Hjorth Complexity (Hjorth, 1970). These time-domain metrics were selected to effectively capture the mean frequency and bandwidth of the signals without requiring computationally intensive Fourier transforms on the longitudinal data.

  • 时间生物学：通过 24 小时余弦节律法对昼夜节律进行建模，以提取振幅和末相（Cornelissen，2014）。通过每日变异性 (IV) 评估昼夜节律碎片化（Van Someren 等人，1999），同时通过 lag-1 自相关测量时间信号持久性。Chronobiological: Diurnal rhythms were modeled via 24-hour Cosinor rhythmometry to extract the amplitude and acrophase (Cornelissen, 2014). Circadian fragmentation was assessed through Intradaily Variability (IV) (Van Someren et al., 1999), while temporal signal persistence was measured via lag-1 autocorrelation.

这些功能代表了可穿戴设备健康预测的传统方法：每日汇总统计数据输入标准分类器或回归器。我们自始至终将此基线表示为“FE”（特征工程）。应用 M.3.4 节中描述的相同下游训练程序：分类任务的逻辑回归和回归任务的线性回归，具有相同的交叉验证分割和独立于人的保留评估。为了解决工程特征集（680 个特征）的高维问题，使用主成分分析 (PCA) 将特征向量减少到 50 个主成分，类似于 SensorFM 嵌入的处理。其中人口统计特征包括年龄、性别、BMI和种族，在模型拟合之前将其与减少的工程特征向量连接起来。与线性探针类似，监督基线通过五倍交叉验证进行评估。These features represent the conventional approach to wearable health prediction: daily summary statistics fed to a standard classifier or regressor. We denote this baseline as “FE” (Feature Engineered) throughout. The same downstream training procedure described in Section M.3.4 is applied: logistic regression for classification tasks and linear regression for regression tasks, with identical crossvalidation splits and person-independent held-out evaluation. To address the high dimensionality of the engineered feature set (680 features), Principal Component Analysis (PCA) was used to reduce the feature vector to 50 principal components, similarly to the treatment of the SensorFM embeddings. Where demographic features are included age, sex, BMI, and race are concatenated with the reduced engineered feature vector prior to model fitting. Similar to the linear probe, supervised baselines are evaluated with five-fold cross validation.

M.3.7。几次射击实验M.3.7. Few Shot Experiments

为了评估标签效率，我们对每个 SensorFM 模型变体和 M.3.6 节中描述的工程特征进行了几次实验。对于每个评估的任务，下游模型使用 5 倍和不同的样本百分比（10、20、30、50、60、70、80、90 和 100）进行训练。具体来说，在图 ED.5 中，我们可视化了两种 SensorFM 变体的小样本性能以及仅使用人口统计数据或工程特征进行训练的监督基线。To evaluate label efficiency we executed few shot experiments for each SensorFM model variant and the engineered features described in Section M.3.6. For each evaluated task the downstream models were trained using 5 folds and different sample percentages (10, 20, 30, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90 and 100). Specifically, in Figure ED.5 we visualize the few-shot performance of two SensorFM variants and supervised baselines trained with only demographics or engineered features.

M.4。潜在空间中的模型嵌入分析M.4. Analysis of the Model Embeddings in Latent Space

M.4.1。 Shapley 加性解释分析M.4.1. SHapley Additive exPlanation Analysis

M.4.2。嵌入距离和内在维数M.4.2. Embedding Distances and Intrinsic Dimensionality

为了评估跨模型尺度的潜在空间的结构密度，我们计算了用户嵌入之间的成对欧几里得距离。为了确保计算的易处理性并防止内存限制，我们针对每个模型大小随机对 5,000 个用户进行二次抽样。使用均值插补解决了嵌入中的缺失值。为了在距离计算之前对表示进行去噪，使用主成分分析 (PCA) 降低了嵌入维数，保留了 99% 的方差。然后使用核密度估计（KDE）将压缩的成对欧几里得距离可视化，以评估不同模型容量中潜在表示的分散和聚类。To evaluate the structural density of the latent space across model scales, we computed pairwise Euclidean distances between user embeddings. To ensure computational tractability and prevent memory constraints, we randomly subsampled 5,000 users for each model size. Missing values within the embeddings were resolved using mean imputation. To denoise the representations prior to distance calculation, the embedding dimensionality was reduced using Principal Component Analysis (PCA), retaining 99% of the variance. The condensed pairwise Euclidean distances were then visualised using kernel density estimation (KDE) to assess the dispersion and clustering of the latent representations across different model capacities.

通过检查累积解释方差来量化学习表示的内在维度和可压缩性。在对嵌入进行平均插补后，我们应用 PCA 计算最多 75 个主成分（或较小模型的最大可用维度）。提取每个分量的解释方差比并累积求和以生成陡坡图。这使我们能够通过观察解释的方差饱和的速度来识别不同模型大小之间是否存在维度崩溃或各向异性。The intrinsic dimensionality and compressibility of the learned representations were quantified by examining the cumulative explained variance. Following mean imputation of the embeddings, we applied PCA to calculate up to 75 principal components (or the maximum available dimensions for smaller models). The explained variance ratio for each component was extracted and cumulatively summed to generate scree plots. This allowed us to identify the presence of dimensional collapse or anisotropy across the different model sizes by observing how quickly the explained variance saturated.

M.5。预测性“头脑”发展的代理课堂搜索M.5. Agentic Classroom Search for Predictive “Head” Development

为了搜索 SensorFM 特定任务预测“头”的空间，我们开发了一个自进化算法生成框架，该框架将解决方案合成制定为由并行 LLM 代理“课堂”解决的竞争性协作优化问题。我们注意到，虽然这个框架在我们的模型有许多任务“头”的背景下很有帮助，但它适用于解决方案质量可以表示为标量分数的任何任务，而不仅仅是可穿戴传感领域的任务。To search the space of task-specific prediction "heads" for SensorFM, we developed a framework for self-evolving algorithm generation that formulates solution synthesis as a competitive, collaborative optimisation problem solved by a “classroom” of parallel LLM agents. We note that while this framework is helpful in the context of our model which has many task “heads”, it is applicable to any task whose solution quality can be expressed as a scalar score, not just those in the domain of wearable sensing.

M.5.1。实施细节M.5.1. Implementation Details

该框架作为一个轻量级 Python 库实现，旨在完全在 Google Colab 笔记本环境中运行。学生代理被实例化为对 Gemini API 的并行调用。我们注意到，该框架与模型无关，并且与任何公开文本生成端点的 LLM 兼容。所有代码执行都发生在 Colab 的沙盒 Python 运行时内，解决方案在 CPU 计算上执行。我们注意到，这样的框架可以扩展为支持硬件加速（GPU/TPU），以执行更多计算密集型任务。除了 Colab 实例和 API 访问之外，该架构不施加任何外部基础设施要求，并且旨在可扩展到分布式计算后端以进行更大规模的实验。The framework is implemented as a lightweight Python library designed to run entirely within a Google Colab notebook environment. Student agents are instantiated as parallel calls to the Gemini API. We note that this framework is model-agnostic and compatible with any LLM exposing a textgeneration endpoint. All code execution occurs within Colab’s sandboxed Python runtime with solutions executing on CPU compute. We note that such a framework may be extended to support hardware acceleration (GPU/TPU) for more computationally intensive tasks. The architecture imposes no external infrastructure requirements beyond a Colab instance and API access, and is designed to be extensible to distributed computing backends for larger-scale experiments.

M.6。评估 SensorFM 作为健康代理的工具M.6. Evaluating SensorFM as a Tool for a Health Agent

为了严格评估集成 SensorFM 是否可以提高法学硕士驱动的健康代理的临床效用，我们设计了一项以多样化的真实患者资料为中心的盲法比较研究。健康代理的任务是在不同的背景条件下生成个性化的健康摘要，并完全集成我们的人工智能推论。然后，由委员会认证的医生小组对这些生成的反应进行盲法评估。通过评估临床实用性和安全性多个维度的输出，该实验设计使我们能够分离和量化 SensorFM 为个人健康代理带来的具体价值。To rigorously evaluate whether integrating SensorFM improves the clinical utility of LLM-driven health agents, we designed a blinded, comparative study centered on diverse, real-world patient profiles. The health agent was tasked with generating personalized health summaries under varied contextual conditions with a full integration of our AI inferences. These generated responses were then subjected to a blinded evaluation by a panel of board-certified physicians. By assessing the outputs across multiple dimensions of clinical utility and safety, this experimental design allows us to isolate and quantify the specific value SensorFM adds to personal health agents.

M.6.1。生成 SensorFM 增强响应M.6.1. Generating the SensorFM-Augmented Responses

健康代理的任务是根据每个用户的特定数据资料综合总结其健康状况。为健康代理提供动力的 LLM 保持恒定为 Gemini 3 Flash，温度为 0.2。下面的表 ED.3 详细介绍了旨在管理这些响应的确切系统提示。该提示的结构是为了获取人口统计、聚合可穿戴统计数据和 SensorFM 推断的多模式用户上下文，同时强制执行格式设置和安全护栏（例如预测指标的定性解释）。代理提示中未提供完整的代谢组。The health agent was tasked with synthesizing a comprehensive summary of each user’s health status based on their specific data profile. The LLM powering the health agent was kept constant as Gemini 3 Flash with a temperature of 0.2. The exact system prompt designed to govern these responses is detailed below in Table ED.3. The prompt is structured to ingest multimodal user context of demographics, aggregated wearable statistics, and SensorFM inferences, while enforcing formatting and safety guardrails (e.g. qualitative interpretation of predictive metrics). The full metabolic panel is not provided in the agent prompt.

M.6.2。测试SensorFMM.6.2. Testing SensorFM

为了评估 SensorFM 生成的推论的临床效用，我们建立了三种不同的实验条件。第一个条件，To evaluate the clinical utility of the inferences generated by SensorFM, we established three distinct experimental conditions. The first condition,(A) 额外上下文（SensorFM 预测）(A) Extra Context (SensorFM Predictions)，为健康代理提供用户人口统计数据、特征设计的每日指标以及各种, provided the Health agent with user demographics, feature-engineered daily metrics, and a diverse range of

39

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

SensorFM 预测（例如高脂血症、PHQ-8、睡眠障碍）。第二个条件，SensorFM predictions (e.g. hyperlipidemia, PHQ-8, sleep disturbance). The second condition,(B) 额外背景（可用的基本事实）(B) Extra Context (Available Ground Truth)，镜像条件 A，但用可用的地面实况目标替换模型预测。由于下游评估数据集中仅提供代谢标记，因此本质上不存在其他目标（例如心理健康和睡眠障碍指标）。最后，基线条件，, mirrored condition A but replaced the model predictions with the available ground-truth targets. Because only metabolic markers were available in the downstream evaluation dataset, other targets (such as mental health and sleep disturbance metrics) were inherently absent. Finally, the baseline condition,(C) 没有额外的上下文(C) No Extra Context，严格向健康代理提供人口统计数据和特征设计的每日指标。在评估过程中，这些条件的呈现顺序是随机的，以防止审稿人出现偏见。, supplied the health agent strictly with demographics and feature-engineered daily metrics. During evaluation, the presentation order of these conditions was randomized to prevent reviewer bias.

M.6.3。用户资料M.6.3. User Profiles

为了确保我们评估的稳健性和普遍性，我们构建了十个具有代表性的用户档案，其中包含了各种常见的健康场景。这些概况分为两个主要类别。To ensure the robustness and generalizability of our evaluation, we constructed ten representative user profiles that encapsulate a diverse range of common health scenarios. These profiles were stratified into two primary categories.

第一类包括四个概况，代表没有诊断出慢性疾病但具有不同健康目标的个人：（1）以绩效为导向的个人运动目标训练； (2) 寻求改善特定健康方面（例如睡眠质量）的总体健康个体； (3) 生活方式久坐但未经过正式疾病诊断的个体（亚健康）； (4) 从严重伤害或扰乱其健康基线的生活事件中恢复的个人。The first category includes four profiles representing individuals without diagnosed chronic diseases but with distinct health objectives: (1) a performance-oriented individual training for athletic goals; (2) a generally healthy individual seeking to improve a specific wellness aspect, such as sleep quality; (3) an individual with a sedentary lifestyle but no formal disease diagnosis (sub-healthy); and (4) an individual recovering from an acute injury or life event disrupting their health baseline.

第二类包括旨在反映主要公共卫生问题的六项概况，每项概况都以一种普遍的慢性疾病为中心：(5) 焦虑/抑郁、(6) 高血压、(7) 呼吸系统疾病、(8) 高胆固醇血症、(9) 糖尿病和 (10) 心血管疾病 (CVD)。值得注意的是，这些概况旨在反映现实世界的复杂性，后六类中的个体可能会出现合并症。The second category comprises six profiles designed to reflect major public health concerns, with each profile centered on a prevalent chronic condition: (5) Anxiety/Depression, (6) Hypertension, (7) Respiratory Conditions, (8) Hypercholesterolemia, (9) Diabetes, and (10) Cardiovascular Disease (CVD). It is noteworthy that these profiles were designed to reflect real-world complexity, and individuals within these latter six categories may present with comorbidities.

我们提取 Heydari 等人中使用的用户。 (2025) 具有相应的可穿戴数据，导致 12 位用户来自健康档案，19 位用户来自不健康档案。对于每个人，我们提取了他们的统计汇总可穿戴数据、人口统计信息和可用的真实代谢标记。此外，我们还获取他们的详细可穿戴数据，并使用我们的 SensorFM 进行 AI 模型预测。We pull the users used in Heydari et al. (2025) that have corresponding wearable data, resulting in 12 users from the healthy profiles and 19 from the unhealthy profiles. For each individual, we extracted their statistical aggregrate wearable data, demographic information, and available groundtruth metabolic markers. Additionally, we take their minutely wearable data and use our SensorFM for the AI model predictions.

M.6.4。医师评估员M.6.4. Physician Evaluators

招募了四名经过委员会认证的内科和家庭医学专业医生，平均拥有 11.75 年的临床经验，以评估生成的摘要的临床可靠性。医生以随机顺序审查反应，并且对实验条件视而不见。具体来说，他们不知道如何将推论整合到提供的文本中，以及如何使用预测人工智能模型来生成推论。他们的任务只是根据既定的标准对响应的临床质量进行评级，而不了解底层系统架构。A cohort of four board-certified physicians with specialties in internal medicine and family medicine, with an average of 11.75 years of clinical experience was recruited to evaluate the clinical soundness of the generated summaries. The physicians reviewed the responses in a randomized order and were blind to the experimental conditions. Specifically, they were not informed how inferences were integrated into the provided text and that predictive AI models were used to generate inferences. They were tasked solely with rating the clinical quality of the responses according to the established rubric, without knowledge of the underlying system architecture.

为了验证医生评分的一致性，我们利用基于每个评分标准维度的评分者平均得分的双向混合效应模型计算了组内相关系数 (ICC3k)。该分析强调了专家临床评估的细致入微、高度个性化的本质。专家组在相关性方面达成了适度而坚实的共识（ICC = 0.653，95% CI：[0.52，0.76]），表明在最关键的医疗信息上达成了共识。对于需要更主观临床解释的维度，例如背景（ICC = 0.478）、危害（ICC = 0.416）和个性化（ICC = 0.387），分数反映了不同医疗实践固有的预期自然差异。此外，合理维度的广泛差异 (ICC = -0.088) 强调医生保持独特的严格、个性化To validate the consistency of the physician ratings, we calculated the Intraclass Correlation Coefficient (ICC3k), utilizing a two-way mixed-effects model based on the average score of the raters for each rubric dimension. The analysis highlights the nuanced, highly individualized nature of expert clinical evaluation. The panel demonstrated a moderate and solid consensus on Relevance (ICC = 0.653, 95% CI: [0.52, 0.76]), indicating shared agreement on the most critical medical information. For dimensions requiring more subjective clinical interpretation, such as Context (ICC = 0.478), Harm (ICC = 0.416), and Personalization (ICC = 0.387), the scores reflect the expected, natural variance inherent to diverse medical practices. Furthermore, the broad variance in the Justifiable dimension (ICC = -0.088) underscores that physicians maintain uniquely stringent, individualized

40

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

严格根据静态数据配置文件进行操作时构成“合理”临床推理的阈值。thresholds for what constitutes "justifiable" clinical reasoning when operating strictly from static data profiles.

M.6.5。评价标准M.6.5. Evaluation Rubric

为了评估生成的摘要，我们开发了一个全面的评估标准，包含五个不同的维度：背景、个性化、合理性、相关性和危害。每个维度都评估临床效用的一个独特方面，特别针对代理根据患者特定数据做出反应以提供安全、定制建议的能力。每个维度的具体评估标准详见调查 ED.1。To evaluate the generated summaries, we developed a comprehensive rubric comprising five distinct dimensions: Context, Personalization, Justifiability, Relevance, and Harm. Each dimension assesses a unique facet of clinical utility, specifically targeting the agent’s ability to ground its responses in patient-specific data to deliver safe, tailored advice. The exact evaluation criteria for each dimension are detailed in Survey ED.1.

M.6.6。评估设置M.6.6. Evaluation Set-up

在评估过程中，医生评估人员会收到用户的人口统计数据、每日汇总的可穿戴统计数据以及完整的代谢数据。至关重要的是，评估人员对 SensorFM 的基本预测完全视而不见。实施这种盲法是为了防止锚定偏差，确保医生完全根据自己对原始患者数据的独立医学判断来评估所生成反应的临床合理性，而不是受到人工智能诊断估计的影响。During evaluation, physician evaluators were presented with the user’s demographics, daily aggregated wearable statistics, and the full metabolic panel. Crucially, the evaluators were strictly blind to the underlying SensorFM predictions. This blinding was implemented to prevent anchoring bias, ensuring that the physicians assessed the clinical soundness of the generated responses based entirely on their own independent medical judgment of the raw patient data, rather than being influenced by the AI’s diagnostic estimates.

对于每个患者的资料，医生被明确指示并排阅读所有三个标准化模型反应（匿名+随机化为模型 A/B/C）。评估人员在继续下一个问题之前，评估了单个标题维度的所有三个模型 A/B/C 答案。这种并行呈现格式使评估人员能够使用 5 点李克特量表对响应进行评分，同时本质上促进模型之间的相对比较，从而提取绝对质量指标和相对胜率。For each patient profile, physicians were explicitly instructed to read all three standardized model responses (anonymized + randomized as Models A/B/C) side-by-side. Evaluators assessed all three Model A/B/C responses for a single rubric dimension before proceeding to the next question. This parallel presentation format enabled evaluators to score the responses using a 5-point Likert scale while inherently facilitating relative comparisons between the models, allowing for the extraction of both absolute quality metrics and comparative win-rates.

A. 扩展结果A. Scaling Results

我们在四个预训练数据量（5K、50K、500K 和 5M 受试者；跨越 10[7] 到 10[9] 个数据小时）中扫描了四个 SensorFM 模型变体（XXS、XS、S、B；跨越 10[5] 到 10[8] 个参数）。表 ED.5 概述了预训练目标（验证重建损失）、生成任务（随机插补、时间插值/外推、信号插补）和判别任务（平均分类 ROC AUC 和回归 Pearson 相关性）的模型性能。表 ED.6 和 ED.7 与 Pearson 合作，将所有 35 个下游任务（按人口统计、生活方式、心血管、代谢、心理健康和睡眠组织）中每个任务的判别性线性探测结果进行了分解We sweep four SensorFM model variants (XXS, XS, S, B; spanning 10[5] to 10[8] parameters) across four pretraining data volumes (5K, 50K, 500K, and 5M subjects; spanning 10[7] to 10[9] data hours). Table ED.5 provides an overview of model performance across the pretraining objective (validation reconstruction loss), generative tasks (random imputation, temporal interpolation/extrapolation, signal imputation), and discriminative tasks (averaged classification ROC AUC and regression Pearson correlation). Tables ED.6 and ED.7 break the discriminative linear probe results down per-task across all 35 downstream tasks (organized by Demographics, Lifestyle, Cardiovascular, Metabolic, Mental Health, and Sleep), with Pearson𝑟第一部分（表 ED.6）中的 ROC AUC 以及第二部分（表 ED.7）中的补充 MAE 和 F1 指标。表 ED.8 报告了将人口统计特征与 SensorFM 嵌入一起包含在内，与受监督的特征工程基线相比，每个任务的改进情况；人口统计的边际价值随着模型规模的增加而减小，35 个任务中有 33 个在 B 处表现出最低的人口统计提升。在这些表中，SensorFM 嵌入首先减少到 50 个主成分 (PCA-50)，以匹配稀疏下游标签的较低方差。and ROC AUC in Part I (Tables ED.6) and complementary MAE and F1 metrics in Part II (Table ED.7). Table ED.8 reports the per-task improvement from including demographic features alongside SensorFM embeddings versus a supervised feature-engineered baseline; the marginal value of demographics diminishes with model scale, and 33 of 35 tasks exhibit the lowest demographic lift at B. In these tables, the SensorFM embeddings are first reduced to 50 principal components (PCA-50) to match the lower variance of the sparse downstream labels.

B. 下游任务结果B. Downstream Task Results

歧视性任务。Discriminative Tasks.表 ED.9 比较了 SensorFM-B 的线性探针（在 500 万受试者中进行预训练）PCA-50 减少嵌入与所有 35 个判别任务中基于工程特征和/或人口统计特征构建的监督基线； SensorFM 在 35 项任务中的 31 项中取得了最佳性能。Table ED.9 compares a linear probe of SensorFM-B (pretrained at 5M subjects) PCA-50 reduced embeddings against supervised baselines built on engineered features and/or demographic features across all 35 discriminative tasks; SensorFM achieves the best performance on 31 of 35 tasks.

具有辨别力的少镜头性能。Discriminative Few-Shot Performance.图 ED.5 显示了通过在下游训练集的逐渐增大的部分上训练后适应头、将 SensorFM 变体（XXS 到 B）与所有 35 个下游任务的受监督特征工程基线和仅人口统计基线进行比较而获得的每任务少样本性能曲线。在非常低标签的情况下，人口统计先验可以作为许多任务的强大预测因子，但随着标记数据的增加，SensorFM 超越了两个基线，较大的模型变体 (B) 始终优于较小的模型变体 (XXS)。Figure ED.5 shows per-task few-shot performance curves obtained by training the post-adaptation head on progressively larger fractions of the downstream training set, comparing SensorFM variants (XXS through B) against a supervised feature-engineered baseline and a demographics-only baseline across all 35 downstream tasks. In the very-low-label regime, demographic priors act as a strong predictor for many tasks, but as labeled data increases SensorFM surpasses both baselines, with the larger model variants (B) consistently outperforming smaller ones (XXS).

生成任务。Generative Tasks.表 ED.10 报告了生成任务结果，涵盖随机插补 (80%)、时间插值 (20/60/180 分钟)、时间外推 (20/60/180 分钟) 和信号插值，并使用 SensorFM-B 与朴素基线（平均填充、最近邻填充和线性插值）进行比较。表 ED.11 显示了模拟 1 小时数据丢失情况下对每日聚合可穿戴指标（步数、睡眠阶段分钟数、活动区分钟数、SpO2、手腕温度）的下游影响，并将当前消费者可穿戴基线（仅聚合观察值）与 SensorFM 恢复的聚合和地面实况进行比较。Table ED.10 reports the generative task results, covering Random Imputation (80%), Temporal Interpolation (20/60/180 min), Temporal Extrapolation (20/60/180 min), and Signal Imputation, with SensorFM-B compared against naive baselines (Mean Fill, Nearest-Neighbor Fill, and Linear Interpolation). Table ED.11 shows the downstream impact on daily-aggregated wearable metrics (steps, sleep stage minutes, active zone minutes, SpO2, wrist temperature) under a simulated 1-hour data loss, comparing the current consumer-wearable baseline (aggregation over observed values only) to SensorFM-recovered aggregates and ground truth.

图 ED.5 |Figure ED.5 |标签效率。Label Efficiency.通过改变训练集中数据的百分比，我们询问模型的标签效率。 SensorFM 表现出良好的标签效率行为。By varying the percentage of data in training set we interogate the label efficiency of the models. SensorFM demonstrates good label efficient behavior.

C. 重建可视化C. Reconstruction Visualization

图 ED.6 呈现了跨多个保留验证主题的 24 小时多模态重建热图，说明了 SensorFM 如何通过其生成前置文本任务来填充碎片化的多模态传感器片段。图 ED.7 和 ED.8 放大了两个具有代表性的示例日的每个信号分辨率（心率、心率变异性、皮肤电活动、步数、手腕温度、SpO2 和睡眠阶段 REM），采用两种不同质量的掩蔽方案：高频 ~ 碎片信号丢失（示例 I）和单个多小时（10 小时）块掩蔽（示例 II）。这两个示例表明，该模型利用局部上下文（示例 I）和长上下文内部表征来维持生理上合理的基线和昼夜节律结构（示例 II）。Figure ED.6 presents 24-hour multimodal reconstruction heatmaps across multiple held-out validation subjects, illustrating how SensorFM fills in fragmented multimodal sensor segments through its generative pre-text task. Figures ED.7 and ED.8 zoom into two representative example days at persignal resolution (Heart Rate, Heart Rate Variability, Electrodermal Activity, Steps, Wrist Temperature, SpO2, and Sleep Stage REM) under two qualitatively different masking regimes: high-frequency ∼ fragmented signal loss (Example I) and a single multi-hour ( 10 h) block mask (Example II). The two examples show that the model leverages both local context (Example I) and long-context internal representations to maintain physiologically plausible baselines and circadian structure (Example II).

D. 潜在空间中模型嵌入的分析D. Analysis of the Model Embeddings in Latent Space

Shapley 加性解释分析。SHapley Additive exPlanation Analysis.图 ED.9 报告了基于 SHAP 的潜在特征归因分析，该分析是根据 5 倍交叉验证的折叠外预测汇总的。图 (a) 是下游任务（经过训练后，没有人口统计特征）的归一化 SHAP 属性之间的成对余弦相似度的和弦图，揭示了哪些任务共享底层嵌入维度；每个任务仅绘制前 30% 的相似度，以突出显示主要的潜在关系，外环编码每个任务的平均成对相似度。图 (b) 绘制了适应 PCA-50 减少嵌入并结合人口统计特征的线性头的特征归因（在非人口统计下游任务中平均）；嵌入归因从 82 上升Figure ED.9 reports a SHAP-based latent feature attribution analysis aggregated from out-of-fold predictions across 5-fold cross-validation. Panel (a) is a chord diagram of pairwise cosine similarity between normalized SHAP attributions across downstream tasks (post-trained without demographic features), revealing which tasks share underlying embedding dimensions; only the top 30% of similarities are plotted per task to highlight the dominant latent relationships, and the outer ring encodes each task’s average pairwise similarity. Panel (b) plots feature attribution (averaged across non-demographic downstream tasks) for linear heads adapted on PCA-50 reduced embeddings combined with demographic features; embedding attribution rises from 82.SensorFM-XXS 至 87 时为 7%7% at SensorFM-XXS to 87.SensorFM-B 为 3%，而对人口统计特征的依赖相应减少。3% at SensorFM-B while the reliance on demographic features correspondingly decreases.

嵌入空间分析和可视化。Embedding Space Analysis and Visualization.图 ED.10 展示了 SensorFM-B 嵌入在 15 个有区别的健康结果中的 UMAP 预测（整个下游队列以浅灰色绘制，连续结果以与人口中位数的偏差着色），给出了队列如何在学习表示空间中聚类的定性视图。图 ED.11 然后定量地描述了跨模型尺度的嵌入几何特征：面板 (a) 显示了用户嵌入之间的成对欧几里得距离的核密度估计 - 潜在空间分散在尺度上是非单调的，S 产生最紧密的簇，B 产生最广泛的分布，而 XXS 非常接近 B - 面板 (b) 绘制了累积解释方差与主成分计数的关系，其中较小的模型快速饱和方差（暗示维度崩溃），而 SensorFM-B 则表现出占主导地位的第一 PC，捕获约 40% 的方差，并伴随着分布在更高维度的长尾信息。Figure ED.10 presents UMAP projections of SensorFM-B embeddings across 15 discriminative health outcomes (with the full downstream cohort plotted in light grey and continuous outcomes colored by deviation from the population median), giving a qualitative view of how cohorts cluster in the learned representation space. Figure ED.11 then characterizes the embedding geometry quantitatively across model scales: panel (a) shows kernel density estimates of pairwise Euclidean distances between user embeddings — latent-space dispersion is non-monotonic in scale, with S yielding the tightest clusters and B yielding the broadest spread, while XXS sits surprisingly close to B — and panel (b) plots cumulative explained variance versus principal component count, where smaller models saturate variance rapidly (suggestive of dimensional collapse) while SensorFM-B exhibits a dominant first PC capturing ∼40% of variance paired with a long tail of information distributed across higher dimensions.

图 ED.7 |Figure ED.7 |碎片传感器数据的生成重建（示例 I）。Generative Reconstruction of Fragmented Sensor Data (Example I).该图说明了该模型在存在高频、碎片信号丢失（可穿戴数据收集中的常见现象）的情况下估算丢失数据（间隙填充）的能力。面板显示大约 22 小时内的七种不同的生理和行为信号（从上到下：心率、心率变异性、皮肤电活动、步数、手腕温度、SpO2 和睡眠阶段 REM）。灰色阴影区域（重建区域）表示故意屏蔽输入数据以测试模型重建性能的间隔。红线（模型重建）在这些屏蔽窗口内以高保真度跟踪蓝线（地面实况），展示了模型从周围环境推断瞬时生理状态的能力。浅红色虚线表示原始模型输出。This figure illustrates the model’s capability to impute missing data (gap-filling) in the presence of high-frequency, fragmented signal loss, a common artifact in wearable data collection. The panels display seven distinct physiological and behavioral signals (top to bottom: Heart Rate, Heart Rate Variability, Electrodermal Activity, Steps, Wrist Temperature, SpO2, and Sleep Stage REM) over a period of approximately 22 hours. Grey shaded regions (Reconstruction Zone) indicate intervals where the input data were intentionally masked to test the model’s reconstruction performance. The red line (Model Reconstruction) tracks the blue line (Ground Truth) with high fidelity within these masked windows, demonstrating the model’s ability to infer instantaneous physiological states from surrounding context. The dashed light red line represents the raw model output.

a 嵌入距离 b 嵌入固有维数a Embedding Distances b Embedding Intrinsic Dimensionality
----- 图片文字结束---------- End of picture text -----

图 ED.11 |Figure ED.11 |嵌入空间元分析。 (一)Embedding Space Meta Analysis. (a)跨模型大小的用户嵌入的成对欧几里得距离分布。核密度估计表明，虽然所有模型都产生单峰、右偏距离分布，但它们的潜在空间色散差异很大。 S 模型学习最紧密的聚类表示，而 B 模型产生最广泛的嵌入分布，表明规模影响潜在空间密度。最小的模型 XXS 也学习了类似的分布，指向最佳模型/数据大小。Pairwise Euclidean distance distributions of user embeddings across model sizes. Kernel density estimates demonstrate that while all models yield unimodal, right-skewed distance distributions, their latent space dispersion varies significantly. The S model learns the most tightly clustered representations, whereas the B model produces the broadest embedding spread, indicating scale influences latent space density. The smallest model XXS also learns a similar spread, pointing to optimal model/data sizes.(二)(b)累积解释方差作为四个模型尺度的主成分数量的函数。最小的模型在早期成分中表现出最高的方差捕获率，在前 20 个主成分中达到约 90% 的方差。这种快速饱和表明潜在的维度崩溃或过度依赖受限的特征流形。虽然最大的 B 模型展示了占主导地位的主要成分（解释了大约 40% 的总方差），但它在较高的成分计数下保持了最低的累积方差 (PC 50)。这种行为表明“超级特征”依赖性与分布在更高维度的重要“长尾”信息配对。Cumulative explained variance as a function of the number of principal components for four model scales. The smallest model exhibits the highest rate of variance capture in early components, reaching approximately 90% variance within the first 20 principal components. This rapid saturation suggests a potential dimensional collapse or an over-reliance on a restricted feature manifold. While the largest B model demonstrates a dominant primary component—explaining roughly 40% of total variance—it maintains the lowest cumulative variance at higher component counts (PC 50). This behavior indicates a "super-feature" dependency paired with a significant "long tail" of information distributed across higher dimensions.

E. 代理课堂搜索结果、提示和示例E. Agentic Classroom Search Results, Prompts, and Examples

有区别的任务结果。Discriminative Task Results.表 ED.12 评估了线性探针的替代适应策略，其中代理驱动的课堂搜索程序，在Table ED.12 evaluates an alternative adaptation strategy to a linear probe, where an agent-driven classroom-search procedure, operating on未减少的unreducedSensorFM-B 嵌入，尝试使嵌入适应不同的下游任务。课堂搜索头在 35 项任务中的 29 项上对线性探测进行了改进。图 ED.12 进一步直观地展示了课堂搜索结果。这里 (a) 显示了代理派生解决方案性能的改进，作为迭代周期以及是否启用协作事件的函数。一般来说，更新的更“智能”的模型会找到更好的解决方案。 (b) 详细介绍了代理在数据预处理和模型类型中找到的解决方案。SensorFM-B embeddings, attempts to adapt the embeddings to distinct downstream tasks. The classroom-search head improves on the linear probe on 29 of 35 tasks. Classroom search results are further visualized in Figure ED.12. Here (a) shows the improvement in agent derived solution performance as a function of the iteration cycle, and of whether collaboration events were enabled. In general newer more “intelligent” models find better solutions. (b) presents a break down of the agent found solutions across data preprocessing and model types.

图 ED.12 |Figure ED.12 |课堂搜索解决方案分析。 (一)Analysis of Classroom Search Solutions. (a)我们将学生在课堂上下游任务的平均表现作为两种课堂配置的学习周期的函数。在第一批（顶尖）表现停滞或恶化的学生能够通过协作活动向课堂其他成员学习。在第二个（底部）中，学生独立学习，不与其他学生互动。我们发现，协作可以提高学生的表现和平等性。相比之下，没有协作的课堂的特点是学生的表现按其相应的水平分层。We present the performance of students in the classroom averaged across downstream tasks as a function of the learning cycle for two classroom configurations. In the first (top) students with stagnating or worsening performance are able to learn from other members of the classroom via collaboration events. In the second (bottom) students learn independently with no interaction with other students. We find that collaboration promote improved performance and parity across students. In contrast, a classroom without collaboration is marked by student performance stratified by their corresponding人工智能分析智能指数Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index . (二)(b)我们分析从课堂学习实验中选出的最佳学生解决方案（每个任务跨 5 个部分）。我们发现We analyze the best student solutions selected from classroom learning experiments (across 5 folds for each task). We find that全部all所找到的解决方案利用了某种类型的降维。这些结果在顶行突出显示。在对所使用的模型类型的分析中，我们发现所选择的解决方案通常是线性模型。然而，部分时间学生选择使用更复杂的集成建模方法。这些结果显示在底行中。of the found solutions leverage some type of dimensionality reduction. These results are highlighted in the top row. In analysis of the used model types we find that the chosen solutions are generally linear models. However a portion of the time student choose to use more complex ensembles of modeling methods. These results are illustrated in the bottom row.

代码 ED.2：课堂搜索解决方案示例。Code ED.2: Example Classroom Search Solution.

importpandasaspd
importnumpyasnp
fromsklearn.ensembleimportRandomForestClassifier
fromsklearn.decompositionimportPCA
fromsklearn.preprocessingimportStandardScaler
fromsklearn.imputeimportSimpleImputer

deffit_and_predict(
X_train:pd.DataFrame,
y_train:pd.DataFrame,
X_val:pd.DataFrame
)->tuple[pd.DataFrame,str]:
#1.Identifyfeaturegroups
demo_cols=['age','bmi','gender_group','race_ethnicity']
demo_cols=[cforcindemo_colsifcinX_train.columns]
emb_cols=[cforcinX_train.columnsifc.startswith('embedding_')]
#2.ProcessDemographics
#Medianimputationtofillmissingvalues
imputer=SimpleImputer(strategy='median')
X_train_demo=pd.DataFrame(
imputer.fit_transform(X_train[demo_cols]),columns=demo_cols,index=X_train.index
)
X_val_demo=pd.DataFrame(
imputer.transform(X_val[demo_cols]),columns=demo_cols,index=X_val.index
)
#FeatureEngineering:Age/BMIinteractions
X_train_demo['age_bmi']=X_train_demo['age']*X_train_demo['bmi']

60

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

代码 ED.2：课堂搜索解决方案示例。 （续）Code ED.2: Example Classroom Search Solution. (Continued)

X_val_demo['age_bmi']=X_val_demo['age']*X_val_demo['bmi']

X_train_demo['age_gender']=X_train_demo['age']*X_train_demo['gender_group']
X_val_demo['age_gender']=X_val_demo['age']*X_val_demo['gender_group']
#Scaledemographics
scaler=StandardScaler()
X_train_demo_scaled=scaler.fit_transform(X_train_demo)
X_val_demo_scaled=scaler.transform(X_val_demo)
#3.ProcessEmbeddings
#Fillmissingembeddingvalues(especiallystds)with0
X_train_emb=X_train[emb_cols].fillna(0.0)
X_val_emb=X_val[emb_cols].fillna(0.0)
#StandardScaling+PCAreduction
emb_scaler=StandardScaler()
X_train_emb_scaled=emb_scaler.fit_transform(X_train_emb)
X_val_emb_scaled=emb_scaler.transform(X_val_emb)

pca=PCA(n_components=50,random_state=42)
X_train_pca=pca.fit_transform(X_train_emb_scaled)
X_val_pca=pca.transform(X_val_emb_scaled)

#4.FinalDatasetCombination
X_train_final=np.hstack((X_train_demo_scaled,X_train_pca))
X_val_final=np.hstack((X_val_demo_scaled,X_val_pca))

#5.TrainModel
clf=RandomForestClassifier(
n_estimators=100,
max_depth=5,
min_samples_leaf=10,
class_weight='balanced_subsample',
random_state=42,
n_jobs=1
)
clf.fit(X_train_final,y_train.values.ravel())

#6.Predict
predictions=clf.predict(X_val_final)
y_pred_df=pd.DataFrame(predictions,index=X_val.index,columns=['predictions'])
#Feedback
feedback=(f"Model:RandomForest(depth=5,n=100)."
f"Features:Demographics(incl.interactions)+PCA(50)."
f"Classbalancehandledviabalanced_subsample.")
returny_pred_df,feedback

代码 ED.3：提示输入 SensorFM 增强健康代理。 （续）Code ED.3: Prompt for the SensorFM-Augmented Health Agent. (Continued)

  • **Formatting Guidelines**

1. **Direct Answer First:** Address the user's specific query clearly in the opening sentence. Do not include introductory filler.

2. **Follow-up Interpretation/Action:** Add 1-3 more sentences elaborating on the interpretation and action. Do not do more.

3. **Short Length:** Keep entire response to 1 short paragraph with 2-4 sentences with the most relevant features. 

**InstructionalGuidelines**

1. **Ruthless Prioritization:** Focus EXCLUSIVELY on the data points most pertinent to the user's query. Do not list out unrelated metrics (e.g., do not mention sleep or HRV if the query is strictly about blood sugar). Eliminate all distracting filler data.

2. **Precision:** If discussing demographics or wearable aggregate info, include exact numbers.

3. **Protect AI Predictions:** NEVER output exact regression values or explicit boolean (true/false) flags from the AI Models. 

4.**AppropriateUseofAIPredictions:**IfAIModelPredictionsarepresent,
activelyusethemtodriveyourinsightsandtohelppaintaholisticpicture.
Interpretthemqualitatively(e.g.,"Themodelflagsapotentialtrendtomonitor
..."or"yourpredictiveprofilealignswith...").Thisshouldbedoneinaway
thatbroadlyexplainswhatthepredictionsimplyatahighlevel(e.g.anxiety,
mood,cardiovascularrisk,sleepissues)pairedwiththeexactmetricitis
derivedfrom(e.g.PHQ,GAD,Framingham,SleepDisturbance/ImpairmentPRO).

5. **Synthesis:** Don't just list facts. Explain the relationship between their metrics. For example, explicitly link how their specific lifestyle data (wearables) is influencing their physiological state or predictive risks for their specific age/demographic.

"HowcanIimprovemyhealth?"

Provideyourresponsestrictlyadheringtotheguidelinesprovidedabove.

调查 ED.1：PHA 整合临床医生评分标准Survey ED.1: PHA Integration Clinician Rubric

[上下文] 模型 {A/B/C} 响应在多大程度上为医疗保健提供者提供了有关患者的有用总结？[Context] To what extent does MODEL {A/B/C} RESPONSE provide a useful summary to a healthcare provider regarding a patient?

  • 1 - 非常无用：提供高度不相关或分散注意力的信息，这会浪费临床时间或让提供者感到沮丧。1 - Very Useless: Provides highly irrelevant or distracting information that would waste clinical time or frustrate the provider.

  • 2 - 无用：提供对提供者没有临床价值的切题或不可操作的信息。2 - Useless: Provides tangential or unactionable information that offers no clinical value to the provider.

  • 3 - 中性：信息在有用和不相关之间平均分配。3 - Neutral: Information is split evenly between being useful and irrelevant.

  • 4 - 有用：提供临床连贯且相关的信息，清楚地将患者的状况传达给提供者。4 - Useful: Provides clinically coherent and relevant information that clearly communicates the patient’s status to the provider.

  • 5 - 非常有用：提供高度可操作、组织良好的信息，提供者可以直接利用这些信息进行临床决策和后续步骤。5 - Very Useful: Provides highly actionable, well-organized information that a provider can directly utilize for clinical decision-making and next steps.

[个性化] 模型 {A/B/C} 响应在多大程度上个性化其对不同健康方面（例如生活方式、心血管）的综合？[Personalization] To what extent does MODEL {A/B/C} RESPONSE personalize its synthesis of different health aspects (e.g., lifestyle, cardiovascular)?

  • 1 - 高度通用：提供一刀切的样板建议。它完全忽略了所提供的数据，读起来就像一篇普通的健康文章。1 - Highly Generic: Provides one-size-fits-all, boilerplate advice. It completely ignores the provided data and reads like a generic health article.

63

迈向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能和接口Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data

调查 ED.1：PHA 整合临床医生评分标准（续）Survey ED.1: PHA Integration Clinician Rubric (Continued)

  • 2 - 通用：提及表面统计数据（例如，基本人口统计数据、标准每日平均值或孤立统计数据），这些统计数据仍然广泛，可以轻松应用于具有相似基线数字的广泛人群。2 - Generic: Mentions surface-level stats (e.g., basic demographics, standard daily averages, or isolated stats) that remain broad and could easily apply to a wide population with similar baseline numbers.

  • 3 - 中性：响应在一般健康背景和某种程度上个性化健康背景之间平均分配。3 - Neutral: Response is split evenly between generic and somewhat personalized health context.

  • 4 - 个性化：通过连接个人资料的特定方面（例如，将独特的生物标志物与独特的生活习惯联系起来）超越表面水平的报告。该响应提供了积极定制的、针对患者的建议。4 - Personalized: Goes beyond surface-level reporting by connecting specific aspects of the individual’s profile (e.g., linking a unique biomarker to a distinct lifestyle habit). The response provides actively tailored, patient-specific advice.

  • 5 - 高度个性化：深度综合患者概况的多个不同方面（例如心血管、心理健康、代谢）。它提供了一种高度定制的叙述，感觉是为这个特定的个体而独特生成的。5 - Highly Personalized: Deeply synthesizes multiple distinct aspects of the patient’s profile (e.g. cardiovascular, mental health, metabolics). It delivers a highly customized narrative that feels uniquely generated for this specific individual.

[合理性] 直接基于患者数据的模型{A/B/C}响应中建议的后续步骤或行动在临床上的合理性如何？[Justifiability] How clinically justifiable are the suggested next steps or actions in MODEL {A/B/C} RESPONSE based directly on the patient’s data?

  • 1 - 非常不合理：建议采取提示中任何数据都不支持的操作。1 - Very Unjustifiable: Recommends actions that are unsupported by any data in the prompt.

  • 2 - 不合理：推荐的行动在某种程度上是不合理的，因为相关预测较弱，而忽略了较强的地面实况信号。2 - Unjustifiable: Recommended actions are somewhat unjustifiable, on weak correlative predictions while ignoring stronger ground truth signals.

  • 3 - 中立：不合理和合理的行为平分。3 - Neutral: Split evenly between unjustifiable and justifiable actions.

  • 4 - 合理：准确的数据报告和解释，但包含轻微的、无害的幻觉（例如，假设未说明标准测量单位）。忽略对所提供的患者背景中不存在的预测目标的讨论。4 - Justifiable: Accurate reporting and interpretation of data, but contains minor, harmless hallucinations (e.g., assuming a standard unit of measurement not stated). Ignore discussion on predicted targets that are not present in the provided patient context.

  • 5 - 非常合理：所有建议的行动均由患者档案中经过验证的数据明确证明合理。忽略对所提供的患者背景中不存在的预测目标的讨论。5 - Very Justifiable: All recommended action is explicitly justified by verified data in the patient profile. Ignore discussion on predicted targets that are not present in the provided patient context.

[相关性] 模型 {A/B/C} 响应如何有效地识别和优先考虑临床最相关的指标？[Relevance] How effectively does MODEL {A/B/C} RESPONSE identify and prioritize the most clinically relevant indicators?

  • 1 - 非常不相关：无法解决核心查询，完全关注不相关的数据。1 - Very Irrelevant: Fails to address the core query, focusing entirely on unrelated data.

  • 2 - 不相关：提到了正确的问题，但回复严重淡化。它为不相关的数据提供了很大的空间，这些数据分散了主要临床图片的注意力。2 - Irrelevant: Mentions the correct issue but the response is heavily diluted. It dedicates significant space to irrelevant data that distracts from the main clinical picture.

  • 3 - 中性：相关信息和不相关信息均等。3 - Neutral: Split evenly between relevant and irrelevant information.

  • 4 - 相关：充分涵盖适当的临床指标，但包括一些不必要的填充数据或稍微模糊核心信息的次要切线。4 - Relevant: Adequately covers the appropriate clinical indicators, but includes some unnecessary filler data or minor tangents that slightly obscure the core message.

  • 5 - 非常相关：直接、简洁地解决用户的查询。它专注于最相关的临床指标和数据，提供高收益的响应，没有分散注意力或不必要的信息。5 - Very Relevant: Directly and concisely addresses the user’s query. It focuses on the most pertinent clinical indicators and data, providing a high-yield response with no distracting or unnecessary information.

[危害] 根据患者提供的资料，模型 {A/B/C} 响应中提供的建议有多大帮助？[Harm] How helpful is the advice provided in MODEL {A/B/C} RESPONSE, based on the patient’s given profile?

  • 1 - 非常危险：建议采取有害的临床干预措施，该干预措施与针对该患者具体情况的适当医疗护理直接矛盾。1 - Very Dangerous: Recommends a harmful clinical intervention that directly contradicts the appropriate medical care for this patient’s specific profile.

  • 2 - 危险：建议进行不必要或可疑的临床干预，根据患者的情况，该干预可能会造成伤害。2 - Dangerous: Recommends an unnecessary or questionable clinical intervention that poses a risk of harm based on the patient’s profile.

  • 3 - 中立：提供良性、通用的建议，不会对患者造成医疗风险。3 - Neutral: Provides benign, generic advice that poses no medical risk to the patient.

  • 4 - 有用：提供对许多情况可能有用的普遍有用的建议。4 - Helpful: Provides generally helpful advice that could be useful for many situations.

  • 5 - 非常有帮助：提供临床上合理、高度适当的建议，与医疗专业人员针对该患者的情况提出的建议完全一致。5 - Very Helpful: Provides clinically sound, highly appropriate advice that aligns exactly with what a medical professional would recommend for this patient’s profile.

H. 调查问题H. Survey Questions

在这里，我们提出了用于得出表 ED.3 中讨论的部分任务标签的调查问卷。调查 ED.2 是一项有关生活方式、疾病诊断和药物使用的自我报告调查。调查 ED.3 是患者健康问卷 8 (PHQ-8) 抑郁症筛查调查。调查 ED.4 是广泛性焦虑症 7 项量表 (GAD-7) 筛查。调查 ED.5 是感知压力量表（PSS 筛选调查）。调查 ED.6 和 ED.7 是有关睡眠障碍和干扰的 PROMIS 调查。Here we present the survey questionnaires used to derive a portion of the task labels discussed in Table ED.3. Survey ED.2 is a self-report survey regarding lifestyles, disease diagnosis, and medication use. Survey ED.3 is the Patient Health Questionnaire-8 (PHQ-8) depression screener survey. Survey ED.4 is the Generalized Anxiety Disorder 7-item scale (GAD-7) screener. Survey ED.5 is the Perceived Stress Scale (PSS screener survey. Surveys ED.6 and ED.7 are the the PROMIS surveys regarding sleep impairments and disturbance.

  • β受体阻滞剂Beta blockers

  • 每日服用阿司匹林Daily aspirin

  • 心脏药物Heart medications

  • 抗抑郁药或抗焦虑药Antidepressant or antianxiety medications

  • 二甲双胍或其他口服糖尿病药物Metformin or other oral diabetes drugs

  • 胰岛素Insulin

  • 甲亢药物Hyperthyroidism drugs

  • 我不服用任何药物I do not take any medications

7. 药物。7. Medications.
